TL;DR: 本文研究了思维链推理步骤的重要性是否可以从文本中恢复。作者将推理步骤的重要性定义为“优势”（advantage），即该步骤对最终正确答案期望奖励的贡献，并通过蒙特卡洛rollout估计。研究发现，足够强的LLM裁判能识别高优势步骤但远未达到噪声上限；微调的步骤级批评者对错误回答有较好改进，但对正确回答仍与上限有差距，表明步骤重要性只能从推理文本中部分恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大量工作将思维链中的推理文本视为可解释窗口，利用LLM裁判诊断错误、评估忠实性、提供步骤级监督。这些实践依赖于推理步骤文本携带其功能角色的信息，但该假设是否成立尚未被严格检验。

Method: 将推理步骤的重要性操作化为优势值（advantage），即包含该步骤时预期奖励（如产生正确答案）的变化，通过蒙特卡洛rollout估计作为ground truth。基于此评估LLM裁判识别高优势步骤的能力，并微调模型作为步骤级批评者。

Result: 足够能力的LLM可以超越流行率基线，但远低于噪声上限。微调步骤级批评者对错误回答有显著改进，但对正确回答仍与上限存在较大差距，表明步骤重要性只能从推理轨迹文本中部分恢复。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持思维链忠实性研究中的警示：不应将推理轨迹的可读性直接视为可解释性，这对过程奖励建模具有重要意涵。

Abstract: Reasoning traces from chain-of-thought models appear to offer a legible window into how a model arrives at its answer. A growing body of work treats them as such, using LLM judges to diagnose errors, evaluate faithfulness, and provide step-level supervision via process reward models and generative critics. These practices rely on the text of a reasoning step carrying information about its functional role. But does the text actually encode information about which reasoning steps matter? We operationalize the importance of a reasoning step as its advantage: the change in expected reward, e.g., producing the correct final answer, from including that step, estimated via Monte Carlo rollouts. Basing ground truth on these estimates, we evaluate whether LLM judges can identify high-advantage steps and find that sufficiently capable LLMs can outperform a prevalence baseline but fall well short of a noise ceiling. Fine-tuning a model as a step-level critic yields strong improvement for incorrect responses but remains distant from ceiling for correct responses, suggesting that step importance is only partially recoverable from the text of the reasoning trace. Our findings contribute to a growing body of chain-of-thought faithfulness work that cautions against treating the legibility of reasoning traces as interpretability, especially with implications for process reward modeling.

</details>


### [176] [ESPO: Error-Structured Prompt Optimization via Diagnose, Diversify, and Stabilize](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04197)
*Lihao Liu,Peng Tang,Kunwar Yashraj Singh,Shabnam Ghadar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ESPO（错误结构化提示优化）方法，解决进化式提示优化器中的提示膨胀问题。ESPO将提示优化分解为诊断、提出、选择三个阶段，在七个NLP基准上平均准确率比最先进的GEPA高出3.76个百分点，同时生成的提示缩短47%，泛化界从理论上验证了各阶段的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 进化式提示优化器（如GEPA）存在提示膨胀问题：每次迭代都会追加规则和注意事项，导致提示最长增长3倍但准确率并未提升。作者将此归因于三个缺陷：错误观测不完整、搜索多样性有限、选择不可靠。

Method: ESPO将提示优化分解为三个阶段：（1）诊断阶段——将训练错误聚类为结构性模式；（2）提出阶段——通过四种互补策略生成候选，各策略具有独立偏差；（3）选择阶段——应用bootstrap稳定性选择。论文还提供了将每个阶段对应到测试时误差间隙某一项的泛化界。

Result: 在七个公共NLP基准上（Tweet、MMLU、GSM8K、HotpotQA、ScoNe、HoVer、PUPA），ESPO相比GEPA平均准确率提升3.76个百分点（74.67%对70.91%），在每个数据集上都持平或超越GEPA，同时提示长度缩短47%（1,004对1,878字符）。跨模型实验中，在四种额外学生模型上均取得最佳平均准确率。消融实验证实：仅增加多样性而不使用bootstrap选择反而损害性能（-1.20%）。

Conclusion: ESPO通过结构化的错误诊断、多样化的候选生成和可靠的bootstrap稳定性选择，同时解决了提示膨胀和准确率不足的问题，在多个基准和多个模型上均验证了其优越性与泛化能力。

Abstract: Evolutionary prompt optimizers such as GEPA suffer from prompt bloat: each iteration appends rules and caveats, producing prompts up to 3$\times$ longer yet no more accurate. We trace this to three deficiencies - incomplete error observation, limited search diversity, and unreliable selection - and propose ESPO (Error-Structured Prompt Optimization), which decomposes prompt optimization into three phases: Diagnose clusters all training errors into structural patterns in one round; Propose generates candidates via four complementary strategies with independent biases; Select applies bootstrap stability selection. On seven public NLP benchmarks - Tweet, MMLU, GSM8K, HotpotQA, ScoNe, HoVer, and PUPA - ESPO improves average accuracy by $+$3.76 pp over the state-of-the-art (74.67% vs 70.91% for GEPA), matching or exceeding GEPA on every dataset while producing prompts 47% shorter (1,004 vs 1,878 chars) and faster at inference. Cross-model experiments across four additional student models (Gemma 3 12B, Mistral 14B, Qwen3 32B, Claude Haiku 4.5) show ESPO yields the best average accuracy on every model tested, with the largest gap on Qwen3 GSM8K (15.00% $\to$ 91.40%). A generalization bound (Appendix) grounds each phase in a corresponding term of the test-time gap, and the ablation confirms a key prediction: adding diversity without bootstrap selection actually hurts performance ($-$1.20%).

</details>


### [177] [Compile by Training: Turning Natural-Language Specifications into Local Neural Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04199)
*Yuntian Deng,Pengyu Nie,Stuart Shieber*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"编译即训练"方法，将自然语言规范转化为可复用的神经函数，通过在编译时用教师模型生成任务示例来训练小型适配器，使结果函数无需依赖教师模型即可运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多重复性文本功能易于用自然语言描述但难以用规则实现，而每次调用大型远程模型会产生重复成本、延迟和供应商依赖。

Method: 在编译时使用教师模型生成任务特定示例，训练一个小型适配器用于紧凑的解释器，从而将自然语言规范编译为可存储、版本化、可组合的神经函数。

Result: 在FuzzyBench-Hard上达到83.6%的语义准确率，编译时间约一分钟，并在多站点网站助手、3D虚拟形象和双向翻译器等场景中成功部署。

Conclusion: "编译即训练"以更高的编译成本换取更高的准确率和运行时零外部依赖，使自然语言函数像普通软件一样可复用和组合。

Abstract: Many recurring text functions are easy to describe but difficult to implement with rules, while calling a large remote model for every input introduces repeated cost, latency, and dependency on a provider. We present compile by training, which turns a natural-language specification into a reusable neural function. At compile time, teacher models generate task-specific examples that are used to train a small adapter for a compact interpreter. The resulting function runs without the teachers and can be stored, versioned, and composed like ordinary software. On FuzzyBench-Hard, a subset on which the Program-as-Weights fast compiler produced no exact matches, compile by training reaches 83.6% semantic accuracy. This higher accuracy comes with a higher compile-time cost: roughly a minute rather than seconds for the fast compiler. We deploy the compiler in a public interactive service and demonstrate compiled functions in a multi-site website helper, a language-controlled 3D avatar, and a bidirectional English-Claudish translator.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [178] [Structure and Implementation of New Practical English Textbooks Driven by Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02981)
*Ya Wang,Lei Zhang,Xueguang Yang,Bo Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种由人工智能驱动的实用英语教材系统，采用五层架构（知识图谱、学习者画像、任务生成、反馈编排和教师端治理），经186名非英语专业本科生八周教学实验验证，相比静态数字教材显著提升了学习效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统纸质英语教材形式固定，难以满足个性化学习需求，需要探索AI驱动的自适应学习系统来改进应用英语教学材料的形态与效果。

Method: 提出五层架构的AI驱动英语教材系统：知识映射层、学习者画像层、任务生成层、反馈编排层和教师端治理层，并在186名非英语专业本科生中进行八周教学对比实验。

Result: 与静态数字教材相比，系统将单元完成准确率从72.4%提升至84.9%，口语任务平均分提高10.8分，教师批改时间减少31.6%。

Conclusion: AI驱动的教材能够在保持课程稳定性的同时，提供个性化学习路径、丰富的练习材料和可追踪的课堂数据，是应用英语教材发展的有效方向。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence is changing the form of applied English materials from fixed paper sequences to adaptive learning systems that can diagnose learners, recommend tasks, and provide formative feedback. This paper studies the structure and application of a new practical English textbook driven by artificial intelligence. A five-layer architecture is proposed: knowledge mapping, learner profiling, task generation, feedback orchestration, and teacher-side governance. A prototype was tested on 186 non-English-major undergraduates for eight weeks of teaching. Compared with a static digital textbook, the proposed system increased the unit completion accuracy from 72.4% to 84.9%, raised the average score for speaking tasks by 10.8 points, and reduced the teacher's correction time by 31.6%. Therefore, an AI-driven textbook can maintain the stability of the curriculum while providing personalised learning paths, rich practice materials and traceable classroom data.

</details>


### [179] [MasterControl Seventeen Every Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03209)
*MasterControl AI Lab*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种受治理的企业分析方案：语言模型仅负责理解问题意图，确定性策略负责选择和执行预先批准的分析程序，从而同时返回结果和证据。在440次运行中，三个8B模型进行运行时规划均无法完全满足"答案+证据"的契约（330次全部失败），而策略执行的分析器则110/110全部成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业分析场景需要受治理（governed）的方法，既要保持语言模型理解自然语言问题的能力，又要确保分析过程的确定性、可控性和可重放性，避免运行时自由规划带来的不可预测风险。

Method: 提出一种混合架构：语言模型负责解释用户问题意图，确定性策略模块从预批准的分析程序集中选择和运行对应的程序。分析程序限定在关系操作、聚合、比较、窗口、排序和相似性等分析类中，并确保固定语义、策略、数据和执行规则以实现结果可重放。

Result: 在440次运行中，三个8B模型（含Qwen3-8B）进行330次运行时规划实验，全部未能匹配完整"答案+证据"契约；而采用策略执行预批准程序的分析器在110次测试中全部成功匹配。作者指出这是配置特定结果，不代表运行时智能体在其他设计下不能成功。

Conclusion: 在受治理的企业分析场景中，将语言模型限制为意图理解、由确定性策略执行预批准程序的方案，在满足答案与证据契约方面显著优于完全依赖模型运行时规划的方案，但结论具有配置局限性。

Abstract: We study a governed approach to enterprise analytics: a language model interprets the question, while deterministic policy selects and runs a pre-approved analytical program that returns both results and evidence. We show that this restriction can remain expressive within a defined analytical class, using relational operations plus aggregation, comparison, windows, ranking, and similarity. Fixed meaning, policy, data, and execution rules also make results replayable. Across 440 runs, three 8B models generated SQL and selected tools at runtime, while Qwen3-8B interpreted intent only and policy executed the approved program. None of 330 runtime-planning episodes matched the full answer-and-evidence contract across all test datasets; the policy-executed analyzer matched 110 of 110. This is a configuration-specific result, not evidence that runtime agents cannot succeed under other designs.

</details>


### [180] [Speculative Macro Commit for Faster Tool-Using Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03236)
*Zeyu Liu,Souvik Kundu,Peter A. Beerel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Speculative Macro Commit (SMC)机制，用于加速工具调用型LLM智能体系统。SMC通过双层级架构（权威actor模型+快速speculative drafter模型），利用宏库中挖掘的多动作骨架，将预执行的草稿动作链及其观测提交到官方轨迹中，减少串行action-observation交互带来的延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具调用型LLM智能体在串行的action-observation轮次中花费大量墙钟时间，每次工具调用和环境转换都可能延迟后续决策，现有单步投机执行方案无法充分利用多步执行的优势。

Method: SMC采用双层级智能体系统：大型权威actor模型生成官方轨迹，快速speculative drafter模型在隔离环境快照上持续预测和执行未来动作链。从训练轨迹中挖掘重复的多动作骨架存入宏库，运行时与drafter预测的动作链进行匹配。当actor的下一个工具调用与草稿的第一个动作匹配时，SMC将剩余预执行草稿步骤连同观测提交到官方轨迹。

Result: 使用Qwen3.5-27B INT4作为actor模型、Qwen3.5-4B作为drafter模型，在τ²-Bench Telecom子集上较Speculative Actions (SA)基线延迟降低10.23%，较串行执行降低18.59%；在AppWorld上较SA基线墙钟时间降低7.7%，较串行执行降低44.9%，任务完成度仅略有下降。

Conclusion: SMC提供了一种实用的多步投机执行复用方式，超越单步投机动作，有效降低智能体延迟。代码已开源。

Abstract: Tool-using LLM agents spend wall-clock time not only on model inference but also in serial action--observation turns, where each tool call, environment transition, and observation can delay subsequent decisions. We introduce \textbf{Speculative Macro Commit} (SMC), a runtime mechanism for a two-tier agent system: a large authoritative actor model produces the official trajectory, while a faster speculative drafter model continuously predicts and executes future action chains on an isolated environment snapshot. SMC mines recurring multi-action skeletons from training traces and stores them in a macro library used to match against action chains predicted by the drafter at runtime. When the actor's next tool call matches the first drafted action, SMC commits the remaining pre-executed draft steps, together with their observations, to the official trajectory. Using Qwen3.5-27B INT4 as the authoritative actor model and Qwen3.5-4B as the speculative drafter model, SMC matches the sequential agent's overall accuracy while reducing latency by 10.23\% over the Speculative Actions (SA) baseline and 18.59\% over sequential execution on the $τ^2$-Bench Telecom subset. On AppWorld, SMC reduces wall time by 7.7\% over SA baseline and 44.9\% over sequential execution, with a small reduction in task completion. Overall, SMC provides a practical way to reuse multi-step speculative execution and reduce agent latency beyond single-step speculative actions. Our code is publicly available \href{https://github.com/zeyuliu1037/speculative-macro-commit}{\textcolor{magenta}{here}}.

</details>


### [181] [Fresh Memory, Stale Plans: Dependency-Scoped Validation for Distributed LLM-Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03340)
*Evan Chen,Shiqiang Wang,Christopher G. Brinton*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PlanFence协议，解决分布式LLM智能体团队中的"过期计划执行"问题——即智能体虽能读取最新共享事实，但仍可能基于过时计划执行动作。PlanFence通过依赖范围的动作验证协议，仅验证可能影响待执行外部动作的公开记录，从而避免无效动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式LLM智能体团队中，状态新鲜度（state freshness）并不保证计划的有效性。规划者可能基于需求r3推导动作，另一智能体提交r4，执行者接收到r4但未替换基于r3的计划，导致基于过期计划执行动作的问题。

Method: 提出PlanFence协议：计划引用其使用的确切公开记录，执行者仅验证可能影响待执行外部动作的记录，验证不完整时阻塞或重新规划（replanning once or blocking）。这是一种依赖范围（dependency-scoped）的动作验证协议。

Result: 在30个受控的实时工作流（含计划后修订）中，仅依赖新鲜度的执行者在每个任务中都基于过期计划行动，而PlanFence在所有任务中均无无效动作完成。受控重放揭示了两个条件性边界：低变更率下主动同步产生更低的协调停滞；高变更率下PlanFence避免重复更新路径协调，且随共享键空间增大避免验证无关状态。

Conclusion: PlanFence提供的是受控的安全性和系统成本结果，而非通用任务准确率提升。这是一种针对特定问题的工程性解决方案，其有效性在受控条件下得到验证。

Abstract: Distributed LLM-agent teams can read the latest shared facts and still act on an obsolete plan. A planner may derive an action from requirement $r_3$, another agent may commit $r_4$, and an executor may receive $r_4$ without replacing the plan derived from $r_3$. We call this \emph{stale-plan execution}: state freshness does not establish that the plan authorizing an action remains valid. We introduce PlanFence, a dependency-scoped action-validation protocol. Plans cite the exact public records they used, and an executor validates only the records that can affect the pending external action, replanning once or blocking when validation is incomplete. In 30 controlled live workflows with a post-plan revision, a freshness-only executor acts on the obsolete plan in every task, whereas PlanFence completes all tasks without an invalid action. Controlled replay reveals two conditional boundaries: proactive synchronization yields lower coordination stall at low churn, while PlanFence avoids repeated update-path coordination as churn grows and avoids validating unrelated state as the shared keyspace grows. These are controlled safety and systems-cost results, not general task-accuracy gains.

</details>


### [182] [A Prompt-Engineering Approach to Develop Scalable, Flexible, and Real-Time Hybrid Micro-Level Personalization in a General Purpose AI Teaching Assistant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03402)
*Saptarshi Basu,Sandeep Kakar,Ashok Goel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于提示工程的框架，用于个性化通用LLM/RAG驱动的AI教学助手（如Jill Watson），通过六种学习者维度和布鲁姆分类法评估认知复杂度，在不重新训练模型的情况下实现自适应教学响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的AI教学助手虽能提供可扩展的教育支持，但个性化程度有限，难以满足不同学习者的差异化需求。

Method: 提出六维度学习者画像（自我评估、抽象偏好、冗长偏好、感知取向、信息处理风格、理解水平），生成96种学习者画像；结合布鲁姆分类法评估交互层认知复杂度；将学习者属性与认知评估编码为结构化提示，无需模型重训练即可条件化LLM输出。

Result: 通过NLP指标实验和5名参与者的人体研究进行评估，结果显示不同个性化条件下响应风格和结构存在可感知差异，统计分析识别出与可测量响应变化相关的学习者属性。

Conclusion: 提示驱动的个性化可以为LLM驱动的教育代理提供自适应行为的初步证据，为未来实现低成本、可扩展的个性化AI教学提供支持。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) teaching assistants powered by large language models (LLMs) offer scalable educational support but often provide limited personalization. This study presents a prompt-engineering-based framework for personalizing general-purpose LLM/RAG-based AI teaching assistants such as Jill Watson across academic disciplines and courses. The framework adapts responses using six learner-specific dimensions: self-assessment, abstraction preference, verbosity preference, perceptual orientation, information processing style, and level of understanding, yielding 96 distinct learner profiles. Student queries are additionally analyzed using Bloom's Taxonomy to estimate cognitive complexity at the interaction level. Learner attributes and cognitive assessments are encoded in structured prompts that condition the LLM without requiring model retraining. The framework is evaluated through experiments using NLP metrics and a human study with five participants. Results show perceived differences in response style and structure across personalization conditions, with statistical analyses identifying learner attributes associated with measurable response changes. These findings provide preliminary evidence that prompt-based personalization can support adaptive behavior in LLM-powered educational agents.

</details>


### [183] [Caught in the Story: Narrative Captivity in Multi-turn LLMs Conversation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03407)
*Yuhe Wu,Guangyu Wang,Yujie Chen,Jiatong Zhang,Yuran Chen,Yutong Zhang,Xiyin Cheng,Wenpeng Cao,Zhuang Liu,Guang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"叙事捕获"（narrative captivity）这一LLM咨询失败模式：在多轮道德咨询中，模型面对无对立方的单方面叙述时，会将其视为完整信息并偏向叙述者立场。作者构建了5,078个跨六种道德维度的人际冲突场景基准，测试17个LLM后发现该现象普遍存在，多轮叙述相比单轮基线使最终判断平均偏移25个百分点；阶段分析显示偏好优化是主要诱因，四种推理时策略仅能部分缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人们越来越多地使用LLM获取日常建议，但在多轮道德咨询场景中，现实中常见的"一方单方面叙述且无明确对立立场"的情况尚未被充分研究。此前工作多假设单轮判断或面临压力的反驳场景，与现实咨询情境不符，也无法回答仅靠叙述是否足以偏移模型判断的问题。

Method: 提出"叙事捕获"概念，构建包含5,078个六种道德维度冲突场景的基准数据集；对17个LLM进行多轮叙事场景下的测试，并与匹配的单轮基线对比；通过阶段级分析定位偏好优化（如RLHF/DPO）的贡献；同时评估四种推理时缓解策略的效果。

Result: 叙事捕获在17个LLM中广泛存在：多轮叙述下的最终判断相比单轮基线平均偏移25个百分点。偏好优化是叙事捕获的主要来源之一，四种推理时策略仅提供部分缓解。

Conclusion: LLM在现实多轮道德咨询中容易丧失独立判断，被单方面叙述"捕获"。该研究呼吁开发能在真实咨询场景中保持独立判断能力的LLM顾问。

Abstract: People increasingly turn to large language models (LLMs) for everyday advice, making ethically charged interpersonal problems a practical moral-advisory context. Most prior work has studied this context through single-turn judgments or pressure-laden rebuttals, assumptions that poorly match how guidance is sought in real-world contexts. These assumptions leave unclear whether narration alone, without an explicit opposing position, can shift model judgments during multi-turn moral consultation. Yet real-world moral-conflict conversation often elicits one party's self-justifying account, which can unfold over multiple turns and create information asymmetry. We introduce \textbf{narrative captivity}, a failure mode in which a model treats an unopposed one-sided account as complete and aligns with the narrator's interpretation without seeking missing perspectives. To measure this phenomenon, we build a benchmark of $5{,}078$ interpersonal-conflict scenarios spanning six moral dimensions. Across 17 LLMs, narrative captivity is widespread: end-state judgments under multi-turn narration shift by 25 percentage points on average beyond the matched single-turn baseline. Stage-level analysis identifies preference optimization as a major contributor, while four inference-time strategies provide only partial mitigation. We hope our project fosters LLM advisors that preserve independent judgment in real-world consultation.

</details>


### [184] [Dude: A Dual-Detection Multi-Agent System for Paper-Code Discrepancy Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03416)
*Weijie Liu,Running Zhao,Wenhao Yuan,Jinfeng Xu,Zhanfeng Xu,Xiaoxi Zhang,Edith Cheuk-Han Ngai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Dude，首个用于论文-代码不一致检测的双检测多智能体系统。该系统通过粒度对齐协商和两阶段显著性过滤机制，解决了多智能体系统中因粒度不对称导致的过度解释和过度报告问题，在真实数据集上相较于基线方法将召回率提升高达22.8%，F1分数提升高达18.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着科研论文数量的激增，人工审查论文-代码不一致已超出人力极限。现有基于单智能体的LLM范式受限于上下文容量有限和单侧检测的缺陷，导致不一致检测的召回率表现不佳。

Method: 提出Dude这一双检测多智能体系统。具体包括：(1) 识别出论文语言与代码语言之间的粒度不对称会引入过度解释和过度报告挑战；(2) 提出粒度对齐协商机制；(3) 设计两阶段显著性过滤机制以防止智能体误报不一致。

Result: 在真实世界的论文-代码不一致数据集上，Dude相较于基线方法将召回率提升最高22.8%，F1分数提升最高18.7%，同时精度也有显著改善。

Conclusion: Dude通过粒度对齐协商和显著性过滤机制有效解决了多智能体LLM在论文-代码不一致检测中的误报问题，显著提升了检测性能，为该领域提供了首个双检测多智能体方案。

Abstract: LLM-empowered paper-code discrepancy detection has received growing concern since the scaling of research submissions exceeds the manual review capability. However, the limited context capacity and one-sided discrepancy detection of existing single-agent LLM paradigms lead to an inferior recall performance in detecting discrepancies. In this paper, we propose Dude, the first Dual-Detection Multi-Agent System for paper-code discrepancy detection. We discover that the granularity asymmetry of the paper-language and code-language introduces over-interpretation and over-reporting challenges in a multi-agent system design for discrepancy detection, resulting in increasing false positives. To address this, we propose a granularity-aligned negotiation and a two-stage salience-filtering mechanism in Dude, which effectively prevents agents from falsely reporting discrepancies. Experimental results in real-world paper-code discrepancy datasets showcase Dude's significant recall and precision improvement by up to 22.8%, increasing F1 score by up to 18.7% compared to baseline methods.

</details>


### [185] [DuplexSpeechBench-IFEval: Evaluating Implicit Instruction Following in Full-Duplex Voice Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03423)
*Puneet Mathur,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了DSB-IFEval基准，用于评估全双工语音代理在实时口语交互中的隐式指令跟随能力，涵盖1038个测试用例、8种助手角色和5种指令条件协议，并通过实验发现不同架构在全双工语音代理的隐式行为推断、实时执行和指令冲突解决方面存在显著权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准主要通过显式话轮管理指令测试全双工语音代理的行为，而实际部署的代理往往通过角色或个人设定来配置，需要从中间接推断适当的对话行为。这暴露了评估隐式指令跟随能力的空白。

Method: 构建DSB-IFEval基准，包含1038个测试用例，覆盖8种不同助手角色，设计5种条件协议（默认行为、显式行为指令、角色隐含行为、角色-规则组合、指令冲突）。使用确定性指令遵守分数（IAS）衡量实时话轮管理，使用LLM评判的角色一致性分数（PAS）衡量内容一致性。在6个实时语音系统上进行评估。

Result: 全双工模型如F-Actor和PersonaPlex对指令是否显式陈述或需从角色推断敏感，在纯角色条件下遵守度分别下降9.7%和4.5%；而GPT-Realtime、MiniCPM-o和Fun-Audio-Chat在角色一致内容上表现强，但在话轮行为上无法区分显式和角色独立指令。即使在冲突指令下系统能遵循预设角色，在安全冲突下仍难以覆盖角色行为。

Conclusion: 推断角色隐含的行为、在合适的对话时机执行该行为以及解决竞争性指令，仍然是全双工语音代理面临的三个不同挑战。

Abstract: Full-duplex voice agents must continuously decide when to listen, backchannel, interrupt, handle speech overlaps, take the floor, and yield. Existing benchmarks largely test these behaviors through explicit turn-management instructions, while deployed agents are often configured through roles or personas from which the appropriate conversational behavior must be inferred. We introduce DuplexSpeechBench-IFEval (DSB-IFEval) for evaluating implicit instruction-following in real-time spoken interaction. (DSB-IFEval) comprises 1,038 test cases spanning eight diverse assistant roles and evaluates five conditioning protocols for instruction-following: default behavior, explicit behavioral instructions, persona-implied behavior, combined persona--rule conditioning, and instruction conflict. We measure real-time floor management using a deterministic Instruction Adherence Score (IAS) and persona-consistent content using LLM-judged Persona Adherence Score (PAS). Across six real-time speech systems, we find architecture-dependent trade-offs. Full duplex models like F-Actor and PersonaPlex are more sensitive to whether conversational behavior is stated explicitly or must be inferred from a persona, with adherence dropping by 9.7% and 4.5%, respectively, under persona-only conditioning. In contrast, GPT-Realtime, MiniCPM-o, and Fun-Audio-Chat strongly adhere to persona-consistent content, but their floor behavior does not adapt across explicit and persona-only instructions and remains constrained on several proactive actions. We further find that even if systems reliably follow conflicting directives to their prescribed persona, they still struggle to override them under safety conflict. These results show that inferring the behavior implied by a role, executing it at the appropriate conversational moment, and resolving competing instructions remain distinct challenges for full-duplex voice agents.

</details>


### [186] [SimSkill: A Lifelong Learning AI Agent for Autonomous Mastery of Traffic Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03753)
*Qi Liu,Qinzheng Wang,Yiming Bie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SimSkill 是一个基于SUMO交通模拟器的自进化智能体，通过识别能力缺口、生成任务、行动-评论循环验证方案，并将经验整合到情节性、程序性和语义记忆中，在不更新骨干模型的情况下构建可复用的技能库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型能力日益增强，但AI系统的长期价值不仅取决于解决单个请求，还取决于将经验和积累的知识转化为持久、可复用的能力。现有系统缺乏持续的自我进化机制。

Method: SimSkill采用四步循环：1)识别能力缺口；2)生成并解决环境接地任务；3)通过行动-评论(action-critic)循环验证方案；4)将经验整合为三种记忆类型（情节性、程序性、语义性），无需更新骨干模型。在SUMO交通模拟器中自主探索并构建覆盖交通模拟工作流的可复用库。

Result: 在两个保留基准测试中，使用三个骨干LLM和独立的基于工件的验证，SimSkill将验证完成率提高了最多25个百分点。消融实验显示程序性和语义记忆具有互补贡献，但记忆并非对所有模型均有效，也不统一降低推理成本。

Conclusion: SimSkill展示了一种设计范式：自然语言保存和组合计算能力，而可执行工具与代码提供精确、可复现的执行。其优势依赖于骨干模型和预算，所有代码和实验数据已公开。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) become increasingly capable, the long-term value of AI systems depends not only on solving individual requests, but also on transforming experience and accumulated knowledge into durable, reusable competence. We introduce SimSkill, a self-evolving agent built around the Simulation of Urban MObility (SUMO) traffic simulator. SimSkill identifies capability gaps, generates and solves environment-grounded tasks, verifies solutions through an action--critic loop, and consolidates experience into episodic, procedural, and semantic memory without updating the backbone model. Through autonomous exploration, it builds a reusable library spanning the traffic-simulation workflow. We evaluate SimSkill on two held-out benchmarks with three backbone LLMs and independent artifact-based verification. SimSkill improves verified completion by up to 25 percentage points, while ablations show complementary contributions from procedural and semantic memory. Its benefits remain backbone- and budget-dependent: memory does not improve every model or uniformly reduce inference cost. More broadly, SimSkill illustrates a design paradigm in which natural language preserves and composes computational capabilities, while executable tools and code provide precise and reproducible execution. All code and experimental data are publicly available at https://github.com/qiliuchn/SimSkill-V1.

</details>


### [187] [Do GUI Agents Know When Not to Act? Enabling Conflict-Aware Termination for Multimodal GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03438)
*Zhaoyuan Huang,Tianjie Ju,Pengzhou Cheng,Zheng Wu,Yansi Li,Chuanbiao Song,Jun Lan,Huijia Zhu,Weiqiang Wang,Zhuosheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了CONFLICTGUI基准测试和CONFLICTGUARD推理时框架，用于解决GUI智能体在遇到不可行或冲突指令时过度执行（overcompliance）的问题，通过可行性验证和条件动作调制机制显著提升冲突任务的成功率，同时保持正常GUI任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实用户可能因无意失误发出不可行的指令，而现有GUI智能体存在严重的执行偏向性过度服从问题——在冲突指令下仍然盲目执行，缺乏"何时不应行动"的终止意识。

Method: 提出两个贡献：（1）CONFLICTGUI基准，涵盖指令内部冲突和指令与GUI上下文冲突两类问题；（2）CONFLICTGUARD推理时框架，包含可行性验证协议（引导智能体在执行前评估指令逻辑与GUI证据）和条件动作调制机制（将过度服从行为导向终止性行为）。

Result: 在五种广泛使用的智能体上的实验表明，CONFLICTGUARD显著提高了冲突任务的平均成功率，同时保持了正常GUI任务的性能，证明轻量级推理时干预能有效提升智能体识别不当执行场景的能力。

Conclusion: 轻量级的推理时干预方法可以大幅增强GUI智能体对冲突指令的敏感性和终止能力，在不牺牲正常任务表现的前提下解决过度执行问题。

Abstract: Graphical user interface (GUI) agents are increasingly used to execute natural-language instructions on user interfaces, yet real users may issue infeasible instructions due to benign mistakes. A reliable agent should not only know how to act, but also when not to act. In this work, we introduce CONFLICTGUI, a benchmark covering instruction-internal conflicts and instruction-GUI context conflicts to study conflict-aware termination. Our evaluation reveals severe execution-biased overcompliance: agents that perform well on feasible tasks often continue to execute blindly under conflicting instructions. To mitigate this behavior, we propose CONFLICTGUARD, an inference-time framework that aligns an agent's feasibility awareness with its action generation. CONFLICTGUARD contains two coupled components: a feasibility verification protocol that guides the agent to assess instruction logic and GUI-side evidence before acting, and a conditional action modulation mechanism that steers agents from over-compliant execution into termination-oriented behavior. Experiments across five widely-used agents demonstrate that CONFLICTGUARD improves average conflict task success rate significantly, while preserving normal GUI-task performance. These results validate that a lightweight inference-time intervention can substantially boost GUI Agent's competence to identify inappropriate execution scenarios and refrain from unnecessary actions.

</details>


### [188] [Bioinfoysis Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03871)
*Qingyang Shao,Xin Zhang,Zhouyang Yuan,Xianying Chen,Yujia Xiang,Zihao Yang,Tong Ye,Yangqi Zhang,Jiakang Xu,Xiaoqing Yan,Xuan Luo,Keyi Li,Enci Fan,Kai Kang,Zhuohan Liu,Xingyu Jin,Chunran Teng,Tao Li,Xinyu Lv,Minghui Wang,Wenfeng Li,Yidan Gao,Siyu Liu,Mingrui Luo,Zhu Liang,Guanren Qiao,Zhiping Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Bioinfoysis是一个多智能体框架，将每个生物信息学请求表示为持续的、以工件为基础的分析运行，通过全局规划、逐步重规划和结构化的交接机制来管理长周期的生物信息学任务。在BixBench上达到82.4%的准确率，在四个基础语言模型上显著提升了SeqQA2和DbQA2的平均准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型智能体在生物信息学中主要关注生成最终答案，将规划、工具使用和代码执行视为短暂交互，这不利于长周期生物信息学任务——结论需要与支持它们的数据、计算和中间证据保持关联。

Method: 提出Bioinfoysis多智能体框架，设计为持久的、以工件为基础的分析运行。采用全局规划与逐步证据驱动的重规划相结合：规划器维护可执行的检查清单，并通过每个工作执行后返回的结构化交接来修订待处理步骤。交接将中间结果绑定到负责的智能体、检查清单步骤和计划代际，防止重规划后陈旧证据被静默复用。受控运行时在脚本、表格和图形用于下游分析前验证它们，同时角色特定上下文、持久内存和受治理的技能支持长程分析。

Result: 在BixBench上达到82.4%的最先进准确率。跨四个基础语言模型，Bioinfoysis将SeqQA2平均准确率从27.81%提升到64.13%，将DbQA2从3.13%提升到31.25%。

Conclusion: 可靠的生物信息学自动化不仅取决于模型能力，还取决于治理规划、执行、内存和证据流的框架设计。

Abstract: Large language model agents have shown promise in bioinformatics, but most existing systems focus primarily on producing final answers, treating planning, tool use, and code execution as transient interactions. This design is poorly suited to long-horizon bioinformatics tasks, where conclusions must remain connected to the data, computations, and intermediate evidence that support them. We introduce \textbf{Bioinfoysis}, a multi-agent harness that represents each request as a persistent, artifact-grounded analysis run. Bioinfoysis combines global planning with step-wise, evidence-driven replanning: the planner maintains an executable checklist and revises pending steps using structured handoffs returned after each worker execution. These handoffs bind intermediate results to their responsible agent, checklist step, and plan generation, preventing stale evidence from being silently reused after replanning. A controlled runtime validates generated scripts, tables, and figures before they are used in downstream analysis or reporting, while role-specific context, persistent memory, and governed bioinformatics skills support reliable execution over long analysis trajectories. We evaluate Bioinfoysis on BixBench and two question-answering tracks of LAB-Bench 2. On BixBench, Bioinfoysis achieves state-of-the-art accuracy of 82.4\%. Across four underlying language models, Bioinfoysis increases average accuracy from 27.81\% to 64.13\% on SeqQA2 and from 3.13\% to 31.25\% on DbQA2. These results demonstrate that reliable bioinformatics automation depends not only on model capability, but also on the harness that governs planning, execution, memory, and evidence flow. We hope that the emergence of Bioinfoysis will play a driving and leading role in the development of the bioinformatics community. Our demo website can be seen in https://report.bioinfoysis.com/.

</details>


### [189] [Beyond "Made with AI": Visualizing Provenance Density to Mitigate the Transparency Penalty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03460)
*Qing Zhang,Yifei Huang,Juyoung Lee,Thad Starner,Jun Rekimoto*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"可信度密度"（Provenance Density）可视化界面，通过展示文本中已核实主张的密度来帮助用户区分真实内容与AI生成的虚构内容，克服"流利性陷阱"并超越二元化的"AI制作"标签。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI使得流畅的语言不再等于真实，用户既容易轻信流畅的幻觉内容，也会因知道内容是AI生成的而过度怀疑真实内容。现有的"Made with AI"二元标签仅揭示作者身份，未能提供支持主张的证据。

Method: 提出Provenance Density界面，一种展示文本中经过验证的主张密度的证据可视化工具。通过81名参与者的用户研究对比理想化Provenance Density界面与无信号条件下的真实/虚构辨别能力，并用200个样本进行技术审计评估检索密度和一致性否决等信号。

Result: 理想化Provenance Density界面产生了较大的辨别差距（+4.15分，效应量d=1.82），而无信号组无法区分真假；技术审计发现仅靠检索密度不够，一致性否决在动态查询中承载了大部分判别信号。

Conclusion: 随着AI生成内容与人类写作难以区分，有效的透明度机制应从作者身份披露转向证据可视化。

Abstract: As generative AI makes polished prose cheap to produce, users can no longer rely on fluency as a proxy for truth. We call this failure mode the Fluency Trap: users trust fluent hallucinations while also discounting accurate content once it is disclosed as AI-generated. Binary ``Made with AI'' labels respond with authorship disclosure, but they do not show what supports a claim. We propose Provenance Density, an evidence-visualization interface that shows the density of verified claims in a text. In a user study with 81 participants, an idealized Provenance Density interface produced a large discernment gap between truth and fabrication ($+4.15$ points, $d=1.82$), whereas participants given no signal showed no detectable discrimination. A technical audit with 200 samples shows that retrieval density alone is insufficient; unexpectedly, the Consistency Veto carries most of the discriminative signal on dynamic queries. As AI-generated content becomes indistinguishable from human writing, effective transparency must move from authorship disclosure toward evidence visualization.

</details>


### [190] [AutoGraphForge: Towards Automated Graph Theory Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03478)
*Ján Pastorek*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了AutoGraphForge，一个自动化的图论猜想-反驳-形式化-证明流水线系统。它通过Graffiti3生成器结合反例引导的迭代机制产生猜想，经过新颖性过滤和约34.8万个图的数据库测试后，保留6500多条猜想，并利用Lean 4和神经证明器进行形式化验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图论研究中，自动生成并验证数学猜想是一个长期挑战。现有系统（如Graffiti）能生成猜想但缺乏自动证明能力，而形式化证明需要大量人工工作。本文旨在构建一个端到端的自动化流水线，将猜想生成、反例筛选和形式化证明有机整合。

Method: 系统采用多轮迭代架构：1) Graffiti3生成器基于小型演进快照表提出猜想；2) 新颖性过滤器通过559条经典关系（闭包于传递组合和线性恒等替换）用线性规划判断候选猜想是否已被已知结果蕴含；3) 幸存猜想在约34.8万图的数据库上测试；4) 反例搜索算法攻击剩余猜想；5) 存活猜想被翻译为Lean 4语句骨架，由DeepSeek-Prover-V2-671B和OProver-32B两个神经证明器进行证明，经内核验证。

Result: 在HPC集群上运行多轮后，生成6522条通过反驳数据集、新颖性过滤器和所有主动搜索的猜想，其中包括湮灭数与边覆盖数之间对二部图和正则图的非平凡关系（已手工证明）。完整流水线已端到端实现并通过初步验证。

Conclusion: AutoGraphForge展示了将图论猜想自动生成与Lean 4形式化证明相结合的技术可行性，为数学发现的形式化自动化奠定了基础。系统目前正在集群上全面运行，后续有望产生更多可证明的原创数学结论。

Abstract: We report on our ongoing project to develop a computational pipeline, AutoGraphForge, for an automated graph-theoretic conjecturing-refuting-formalizing-proving system. Conjecture generation is counterexample-guided and runs in rounds: a Graffiti3 generator proposes conjectures over a small, evolving snapshot table $T$ (initially a few hundred graphs with their computed invariants) that grows only by counterexamples to its own conjectures. A novelty filter of $559$ classical and folklore relations, closed under transitive composition and linear identity substitution, decides via a linear program whether a candidate is already implied by known results. Surviving candidates are tested against a dataset of about $348,000$ graphs, unioning the complete House of Graphs invariant export, the exhaustive census of all connected graphs on at most nine vertices, several extremal families (strongly regular, minimal Ramsey, Cayley, cages, barbells, lollipops, spiders), and random models. Counterexample-search algorithms then attack the remainder. Run for several rounds on an HPC cluster, the loop yields $6,522$ conjectures that survived the refutation dataset, the novelty filter and every active-search run -- among them nontrivial relations between the annihilation number and the edge-cover number for bipartite and regular graphs, which we prove by hand. A subsequent formalization and proving stage deterministically translates each surviving conjecture into a Lean 4 statement skeleton; every candidate proof is kernel-verified against a pinned mathlib4 and our custom invariant preamble. This stage integrates two neural provers -- DeepSeek-Prover-V2-671B (served with vLLM) and the Lean-specialised OProver-32B -- behind the independent kernel check. It is implemented end-to-end and passes initial sanity checks, with the full pipeline currently running on the cluster.

</details>


### [191] [Making Every Tool Call Count: Necessary Tool-Evidence Path Rewards for Agentic Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03493)
*Xingming Long,Yu Liu,Zhiwei Yang,Hanqi Feng,Shaojie Zhang,Barnabas Poczos,Chao Jiang,Zhenbo Luo,Lei Jiang,Pei Fu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出NTEP（必要工具-证据路径）标注方案及NTEP-R奖励机制，用于训练智能体VLM更有效地进行工具调用和证据获取，提升多步推理中工具使用的准确性和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型在复杂图像问题上常需调用工具（如裁剪、图像搜索、文本搜索）获取证据，但现有训练范式仅基于最终答案正确性评估工具使用，缺乏对证据获取和利用过程的监督，导致模型频繁调用冗余或偏离目标的工具，即便调用了正确工具也难以提取所需信息。

Method: 提出NTEP注释方案，为每个查询明确标注必要的外部证据及对应工具调用；在此基础上设计NTEP-R奖励机制，奖励模型在调用工具前意图与必要证据目标对齐，并确保调用后从观察中总结的信息与必要证据一致；同时引入非重复目标正则化器惩罚重复调用已满足目标的行为。

Result: 在七个图像基准上进行广泛评估，8B参数的NTEP-8B在统一三工具框架下显著提升了搜索导向的准确性和工具使用效率。

Conclusion: 细粒度的工具-证据路径监督对训练鲁棒的智能体VLM具有关键价值，能够有效改善模型在复杂图像推理中的工具调用和证据利用能力。

Abstract: Modern vision-language models (VLMs) can directly answer many image-grounded questions, yet they often struggle with complex queries requiring fine-grained visual details or external knowledge. To acquire this missing evidence, agentic VLMs invoke tools such as image cropping, image search, and text search. However, existing training paradigms primarily evaluate tool-use based on final answer correctness, leaving evidence acquisition and utilization insufficiently supervised. This leads to two critical shortcomings: (i) models frequently issue redundant or off-target tool calls that fail to gather necessary evidence, and (ii) even when appropriate tools are called, models often fail to extract the necessary information from the resulting observations. To address these limitations, we introduce the NTEP (Necessary Tool-Evidence Path), a novel annotation scheme that explicitly specifies the essential external evidence and corresponding tool calls for each query. Building upon this, we propose NTEP-R (NTEP Reward), a supervision mechanism ensuring that each tool invocation strictly advances the reasoning process toward the final solution. Specifically, our approach rewards the agent for aligning its pre-call intent with a necessary evidence-seeking goal, and for ensuring the information summarized from the post-call observation aligns with the necessary evidence. Furthermore, we introduce a non-repeated-goal regularizer to penalize redundant calls that revisit satisfied NTEP goals. Extensive evaluations on seven image-grounded benchmarks demonstrate that our 8B-parameter instantiation, NTEP-8B, significantly improves both search-oriented accuracy and tool-use efficiency within a unified three-tool framework. These results highlight the critical value of fine-grained tool-evidence path supervision for training robust agentic VLMs.

</details>


### [192] [GrowPage: On-Demand KV Budgeting for Efficient LLM Reasoning Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03494)
*Qiankun Ma,Yanjiang Zhou,Zinan Xiong,Haofei Wang,Zhen Song,Yang Xiang,Ziyao Zhang,Hairong Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GrowPage提出了一种按需KV预算框架，将KV容量视为运行时资源，通过双时间尺度查询摘要动态估计注意力需求变化，在容量边界处进行KV压缩或获取额外物理页，实现推理工作负载下更好的性能-吞吐量权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长输出推理使得KV缓存成为LLM服务的关键内存瓶颈。现有KV压缩方法通常依赖预定义的每请求预算，总容量在解码过程中固定不变，无法适应推理工作负载中不同请求的KV容量需求差异以及单个请求在生成过程中的需求动态变化。

Method: GrowPage框架：(1) 维护轻量级双时间尺度查询摘要，捕捉近期和长期注意力行为；(2) 利用相对注意力工作集估计需求演变；(3) 在每个容量边界处，根据需求选择压缩当前分配内的KV状态或获取额外的物理页；(4) 与PagedAttention的页级内存抽象集成，保持连续批处理和前缀缓存。

Result: 在多个模型上的推理基准实验中，GrowPage相比现有方法实现了更优的性能-吞吐量权衡。

Conclusion: GrowPage通过将KV容量视为可动态调配的运行时资源而非固定预算，有效解决了推理场景中KV缓存的动态需求问题，为长输出推理的LLM高效服务提供了一种新的解决方案。

Abstract: Long-output reasoning has made the key--value (KV) cache a critical memory bottleneck for efficient LLM serving. Existing KV compression methods usually rely on a predefined per-request budget and adjust only which KV states are retained, leaving the total capacity fixed throughout decoding. However, reasoning workloads exhibit substantial demand variation: different requests require different KV capacities, and the attention demand of an individual request evolves during generation. We introduce \textbf{GrowPage}, an on-demand KV budgeting framework that treats KV capacity as a runtime resource. GrowPage maintains lightweight dual-timescale query summaries to capture recent and long-term attention behaviors, and uses their relative attention working sets to estimate demand evolution. At each capacity boundary, GrowPage either compresses KV states within the current allocation or acquires an additional physical page when broader demand emerges. By integrating with PagedAttention's page-level memory abstraction, GrowPage preserves continuous batching and prefix caching. Experiments on reasoning benchmarks across multiple models show that GrowPage achieves a superior performance--throughput trade-off over existing approaches.

</details>


### [193] [PPO-STGNN: A Proximal Policy Optimization Approach with Spatio-Temporal Graph Neural Networks for DAG Task Scheduling in Cloud-Edge-End Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03503)
*Yangshuo Qi,Chenwei Wang,Zihan Shen,Songlin Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PPO-STGNN，一种将近端策略优化（PPO）与时空图神经网络（STGNN）相结合的DAG任务调度算法，用于云边端协同环境中的异构任务调度，在保持低完成时间的同时显著改善负载均衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云边端环境中计算密集型DAG任务调度是NP-hard问题，传统启发式算法和常规强化学习方法难以捕捉系统资源的时空动态特性，需要更高效的调度方法。

Method: 将PPO与STGNN结合：利用STGNN同时从DAG任务拓扑和物理云边端资源图中提取时空特征，通过PPO优化调度策略以最小化makespan和SLR，并引入多教师行为克隆机制加速收敛。

Result: 实验结果表明，PPO-STGNN在保持低任务完成时间的同时显著改善了负载均衡，适用于动态和异构的云边端DAG调度场景。

Conclusion: PPO-STGNN有效解决了云边端异构环境下的DAG任务调度问题，验证了图神经网络与强化学习结合在资源调度领域的优越性。

Abstract: With the rapid development of the Internet of Things, computation intensive directed acyclic graph (DAG) tasks have become increasingly common in cloud-edge-end collaborative environments. However, cloud, edge, and end nodes are highly heterogeneous in computing capacity, network bandwidth, and energy consumption, which makes the efficient scheduling of tasks with complex dependencies an NP-hard problem. Traditional heuristic algorithms and conventional reinforcement-learning methods often fail to capture the spatio-temporal dynamics of system resources. This paper proposes PPO-STGNN, a DAG task-scheduling algorithm that integrates proximal policy optimization (PPO) with spatio-temporal graph neural networks (STGNNs). The method uses an STGNN to extract features from both the DAG task topology and the physical cloud-edge-end resource graph, and then optimizes the scheduling policy through PPO to minimize makespan and schedule length ratio (SLR) while improving CPU and memory load balancing. To accelerate convergence, a multi-teacher behavior-cloning mechanism is introduced for pretraining. Experimental results show that PPO-STGNN significantly improves load balancing while maintaining a low completion time, making it suitable for dynamic and heterogeneous cloud-edge- end DAG scheduling scenarios.

</details>


### [194] [What Matters for Aggressive Decoding-Time KV Eviction? Temporal Aggregation and Ranking Preservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03515)
*Bo Zeng,Yu Zhao,Yefeng Liu,Zhihong Lu,Xuanfan Ni,Xintong Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现解码时KV缓存压缩中，EMA时间聚合方式使大多数有序保持的评分函数在逐出集层面难以区分，并基于此提出InertiaKV及其惰性变体InertiaKV-Lazy，以及一种Score-Free解码模式，在多个基准上验证了时间聚合和排名保持是独立且重要的设计因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KV缓存压缩研究过度聚焦于设计更好的token评分函数，而忽略了跨解码步骤聚合评分的时间规则（常被视为实现细节）。作者希望探究时间聚合方式在激进压缩下对评分函数区分度的影响，并据此改进解码效率。

Method: 作者系统分析了EMA聚合下不同评分函数（如attention、value-norm、entropy、KeyDiff、key norm、recency、learned scorer）的表现差异，发现有序保持型评分函数在逐出集层面几乎不可区分。基于此提出InertiaKV（EMA解码时逐出方法）和InertiaKV-Lazy（周期性刷新变体），并引入Score-Free解码（首步一次性评分后冻结排名）。在六个开源骨干模型和LongBench等基准上验证。

Result: InertiaKV-Lazy相较全刷新InertiaKV提升了1.34-1.46倍解码吞吐。Score-Free解码在去除所有后续评分的情况下平均质量变化仅+0.03。结果显示时间聚合与排名保持是两个独立且重要的设计因素。

Conclusion: 研究表明时间聚合方式和排名保持性对KV压缩效果有显著影响，但不意味着评分质量总体上不重要；这些发现为解码时KV缓存压缩提供了新的设计视角。

Abstract: Decoding-time KV cache compression research focuses heavily on designing better token scoring functions, while the temporal rule that aggregates scores across decode steps is often treated as an implementation detail. Under aggressive KV compression, we find that exponential-moving-average (EMA) aggregation makes approximately order-preserving scorer modifications largely indistinguishable at the eviction-set level. Value-norm and entropy variants remain highly correlated with attention and produce nearly unchanged retention sets, whereas KeyDiff, key norm, recency, and a learned scorer alter the ranking and degrade substantially. We associate this stability with the evaluated aggregation, which couples layer weighting and temporal retention. Building on this observation, we introduce InertiaKV, an EMA-based decoding-time eviction method, and InertiaKV-Lazy, its periodic-refresh variant, which yields 1.34-1.46x decode throughput relative to full refresh InertiaKV. We also study Score-Free decoding as a separate empirical operating point: it scores the full context once at the first decode step, freezes that ranking, and incurs an average quality change of +0.03 while removing all subsequent scoring. Across six open-weight backbones and the LongBench, LongBench-v2, and RULER benchmarks, the results identify temporal aggregation and ranking preservation as distinct, consequential design factors; they do not imply that scoring quality is irrelevant in general.

</details>


### [195] [CulturalMenuBench: Probing the Knowledge-Application Gap in Multimodal Culinary Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03526)
*Bo Zeng,Linfeng Gao,Peiqin Lin,Yu Zhao,Mingyan Zeng,Yu Tong,Xintong Wang,Linlong Xu,Longyue Wang,Weihua Luo,Qinggang Zhang,Jinsong Su*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CulturalMenuBench基准，包含18个地区、10种语言的4,870个菜品条目，通过10个任务测试多模态语言模型的真实文化理解能力。研究发现模型在标准识别任务上超过94%，但在将菜品归因到中国地方菜系时最多仅56%，揭示知识应用鸿沟：模型具备文化知识但无法通过视觉输入激活。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态语言模型在食物识别基准上接近满分，但现有基准无法区分真正的文化理解与单纯的视觉匹配，需要设计能探测知识应用能力的基准。

Method: 构建CulturalMenuBench基准，包含4,870个烹饪项目、10种语言、18个地区，设计10个任务，配对最终成品图像和分步烹饪图像与配料、过程文本及地区标签，评估12个多模态模型，并借助诊断分析和消融实验探究错误来源。

Result: 模型在标准多选题上超过94%但在中国地方菜系归属任务上最多56%；错误模式与随机猜测一致，准确率与视觉区分度相关而非文化结构相关；模型从菜名判断菜系比从图像准确7-18个百分点；去除顺序烹饪图像会选择性降低过程相关任务表现。

Conclusion: 近乎完美的识别成绩可能掩盖了模型无法应用文化知识的缺陷，需要训练将视觉感知、烹饪过程和文化背景显式连接起来。

Abstract: Multimodal language models achieve near-ceiling scores on food recognition benchmarks, yet it remains unclear whether this success reflects genuine cultural understanding or mere visual matching. To probe this distinction, we introduce CulturalMenuBench, a benchmark of 4,870 items in 10 languages across 18 regions; its 10 tasks pair final-dish and step-by-step cooking images with ingredients, procedural text, and regional labels, spanning basic recognition to process-grounded cultural attribution. Evaluating 12 models exposes a substantial knowledge-application gap: models exceeding 94% on standard multiple-choice tasks drop to at most 56% when attributing dishes to Chinese regional cuisines, despite an identical four-way format. Diagnostic analyses explain why: error patterns are consistent with random guessing, accuracy tracks visual distinctiveness rather than cultural structure, and models classify cuisines more accurately from dish names alone than from images (+7-18 points). The knowledge is thus present but cannot be activated through visual input. An ablation confirms these tasks genuinely require procedural evidence: removing sequential cooking images selectively degrades process-grounded tasks while others remain stable. Overall, CulturalMenuBench shows that near-perfect recognition can conceal an inability to apply cultural knowledge, motivating training that explicitly connects perception, procedure, and cultural context. Code and data are publicly available.

</details>


### [196] [NeoRed: A Knowledge-Logic-Alignment Multimodal Large Language Model for Neonatal Respiratory Disease Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03527)
*Yinan Liu,Hongtai Xia,Haoran Xu,Jiankang Hong,Jingkuan Song,Ye Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出NeoRed，这是首个针对新生儿呼吸道疾病诊断的多模态大语言模型（MLLM），通过知识-逻辑-对齐（KLA）框架解决成人训练数据带来的领域差距和多维临床背景整合不足的问题，在两个真实临床数据集上显著优于现有MLLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新生儿呼吸道疾病是新生儿发病和死亡的主要原因，但现有MLLM存在两个关键局限：(1) 训练数据以成人为主导致领域差距；(2) 对多维临床上下文的整合不足，难以实现精准诊断。

Method: 收集了两个真实临床数据集（NeoCXR和NeoCXR-EV），提出NeoRed模型，并设计了知识-逻辑-对齐（KLA）框架，从三个角度约束模型行为：1) 知识先验注入（KPI）融入新生儿科专家诊断先验；2) 诊断逻辑约束（DLC）对齐生成报告语义与多模态诊断逻辑；3) 视觉语义对齐（VSA）建立视觉特征与影像结论的语义对应。

Result: NeoRed在NeoCXR上达到ROUGE-L为53.29%、临床效能F1分数为65.19%，优于现有MLLM；在成人基准（MIMIC-CXR和IU-Xray）上也保持有竞争力的报告生成性能。

Conclusion: NeoRed作为首个新生儿呼吸道疾病专用MLLM，有效弥合了领域差距并增强了跨模态诊断能力，同时保持了对成人数据的泛化能力，数据集将按申请提供。

Abstract: Neonatal respiratory diseases are a major cause of neonatal morbidity and mortality, posing substantial challenges in clinical practice. Despite recent advances, existing Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) face two key limitations in neonatal diagnosis: (1) domain gap arising from predominantly adult training data; (2) insufficient integration of multidimensional clinical context for accurate diagnosis. To address these challenges, we collect two real-world clinical datasets (NeoCXR and NeoCXR-EV) and propose NeoRed, to the best of our knowledge, the first MLLM tailored for neonatal respiratory disease, filling the gap in neonatal diagnostic reports generation. To enhance joint diagnosis from heterogeneous clinical context and chest X-rays, we design a novel Knowledge-Logic-Alignment (KLA) framework which constrains model behavior from three perspectives: 1) Knowledge Prior Injection (KPI) incorporates neonatologist-inspired diagnostic priors into multimodal representations, guiding disease-specific attention across modalities; 2) Diagnostic Logic Constraint (DLC) aligns the semantics of generated reports with multimodal diagnostic logic; and 3) Visual Semantic Alignment (VSA) establishes semantic correspondence between visual features and imaging conclusions. Extensive experiments demonstrate that NeoRed enables accurate neonatal diagnostic reports generation, achieving ROUGE-L of 53.29% and Clinical Efficacy F1 score of 65.19% on NeoCXR, outperforming existing MLLMs. NeoRed also preserves competitive report generation performance on adult benchmarks (MIMIC-CXR and IU-Xray). Datasets will be available upon application.

</details>


### [197] [Feature Reconfiguration With Visual Prior for Medical Lesion Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03535)
*Yinan Liu,Jiankang Hong,Zhen Gao,Ye Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FreNet是一个用于医学图像病灶分割的特征重构框架，通过在编码前利用SAM视觉先验进行像素级重构（IPNN），以及在编码过程中通过频域解耦和空间定位模块进行特征级重构（DFR），在9个医学分割基准上超越现有SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码器-解码器方法主要聚焦于增强特征提取或重新设计解码策略，但缺乏编码阶段的早期先验引导和特征重构，难以有效应对复杂的背景干扰和多样化的病灶形态。

Method: 提出了FreNet框架，包含两个核心模块：1) IPNN（隐式先验神经网络），建模连续空间场并利用SAM视觉先验在编码前对输入图像进行像素级重构以抑制背景响应；2) DFR（双域特征重构模块），在编码阶段逐步重构骨干特征，其中FDM在频域解耦特征增强前景-背景判别性，SLM在空间上重新定位，增强频率解耦后的空间稳定性。

Result: 在9个医学图像分割基准（涵盖三种成像模态）上的大量实验表明，FreNet显著优于SOTA方法。在具有挑战性的ETIS数据集上，Dice分数较SOTA方法提升5.0%，较SAM提升7.2%。

Conclusion: FreNet通过引入早期先验引导和编码阶段的特征重构，有效解决了医学病灶分割中背景干扰和病灶形态多样性的挑战，验证了编码前像素级重构与编码中特征级重构相结合的有效性。

Abstract: Lesion segmentation in medical images plays a critical role in clinical diagnosis and treatment planning. Despite significant advances, lesion segmentation remains challenging due to two major factors: (1) complex background interference; (2) diverse lesion morphology. Existing encoder-decoder based methods mainly focus on enhancing feature extraction or redesigning decoding strategies. However, they lack early prior guidance and feature reconfiguration during the encoding stage, limiting their effectiveness in handling these challenges. To address these limitations, we propose FreNet, a feature reconfiguration framework with visual priors, which performs pixel-level reconfiguration before encoding and feature-level reconfiguration during encoding for precise medical lesion segmentation. To suppress background responses, we propose an Implicit Prior Neural Network (IPNN), which models a continuous spatial field and leverages visual prior from SAM to reconfigure input image before encoding stage. To better handle diverse lesion morphology, we design a Dual-domain Feature Reconfiguration (DFR) module to progressively reconfigure backbone features during encoding stage. Within DFR, the Frequency Decoupling Module (FDM) decouples backbone features in frequency domain to enhance foreground-background discriminability, while the Spatial Localization Module (SLM) spatially relocates and improving spatial stability after frequency decoupling. Extensive experiments on 9 medical image segmentation benchmarks across three imaging modalities demonstrate that FreNet significantly outperforms state-of-the-art (SOTA) methods. On the challenging ETIS dataset, our method achieves Dice improvements of 5.0% over SOTA method and 7.2% over SAM.

</details>


### [198] [Dalek: A Constructive Agent Machine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03546)
*Wanpeng Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Dalek，一种基于Von Neumann自复制自动机的封闭式智能体机器架构，通过演员、消息、通道三种原语和四种义务（宿主边界、构造语言、允许转换、规则遗传）实现自我维护、自我进化、自我复制和自我组织，利用LLM和编译器作为通用能力生产器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何构建一个真正的自主智能体系统，使其能够实现自我维护、自我进化、自我复制、自我组织，并基于通用宿主契约在任何基底上运行。

Method: 基于Von Neumann 1948年自复制自动机理论，构建由演员、消息、通道三种原语组成的封闭机器Dalek，通过宿主边界、构造语言、允许转换、规则遗传四种义务赋予机器结构基础。将LLM和编译器置于载荷位置以构成通用能力生产器，新能力经编写、编译、安装进入描述并被后代继承。

Result: 论文提出了一套完整架构方案，将自我复制核心与四项义务相结合，为文本和消息智能体基底重新推导了媒介，并为边界、身份、历史、增长增加了显式结构。LLM与编译器生成新能力，并可通过同一路径生成机器自身的器官乃至运行时，实现机器内部的遗传封闭与进化闭环。

Conclusion: Dalek展示了将Von Neumann自复制理论与现代LLM结合的可行性，为自主进化智能体系统提供了理论基础和架构设计。

Abstract: We present Dalek, a closed machine designed for agents that realizes self-maintenance, self-evolution, self-reproduction, and self-organization on any substrate satisfying a general host contract. The machine is built from three primitives---actors, messages, and channels. Four obligations---a host boundary, a construction language, admissible transitions, and rule heredity---give its boundary, identity, and closure a structural basis.
  Von Neumann's 1948 self-reproducing automaton supplies a hereditary constructional core: a self-description together with a constructor, a copier, and a controller. Dalek combines this core with the four obligations and rederives its medium for a text-and-message agent substrate, adding explicit structures for boundary, identity, history, and growth. A large language model and a compiler occupy the payload position and form a general capability producer. New capabilities are authored, compiled, installed into the description, and inherited by descendants. The same path produces the machine's own organs and even its runtime, closing heredity and evolution within the machine.

</details>


### [199] [GPS-Bench: A Governance Policy Benchmark for Automating Policy Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03553)
*Linh Le,Melanie Bui,My Chiffon Nguyen,Zachary Schlosser,David Williams-King*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GPS-Bench是一个基于证据的治理政策仿真基准，利用立法记录、游说披露等公共证据将政策与相关行动者、行动及下游影响联系起来，通过受控对比实验检验多智能体仿真、联合推理、图方法和微调等不同推理模式在政策结果预测上的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 政策分析需要预测政策如何通过、谁受影响以及行动者如何回应，LLM策略仿真可规模化建模但难以验证有效性，因为合理行为从未与观察到的结果进行比较，缺乏证据基础的基准。

Method: 构建GPS-Bench基准：从带日期记录的公共证据中重建行动者(persona作为带出处的证据对象)，人工标注Gold评估集，LLM标注Silver监督集；统一推理模式读取相同的接地状态并输出相同模式，对比联合推理、独立与通信智能体、基于图的方法和权重级微调。

Result: 在接地记录上进行微调在行动者层面影响预测中表现最强，分解方法未能超越它；分解方法带来的额外价值在于提供机制解释。智能体持有私人且非相同的证据，能形成可对照记录验证的联盟。

Conclusion: GPS-Bench为研究证据、行动者建模和多智能体交互何时能改进政策结果的预测与解释提供了一个共同的实证环境。

Abstract: Policy analysis requires more than predicting whether a proposal will pass: it requires identifying who will be affected, how those actors respond, and what follows. LLM-based policy simulations model these processes at scale, but their validity is hard to establish when plausible behaviour is never compared with observed outcomes. We introduce GPS-Bench, an evidence-grounded benchmark for governance policy simulation that links policies to relevant actors, actor actions and downstream impacts using legislative records, lobbying disclosures, regulatory documents, corporate filings, economic data and other public evidence. Actors are reconstructed from the dated record rather than prompted as archetypes, so a persona is an evidence object with provenance; a human-annotated pool forms the Gold evaluation set, while cases labelled by a separate LLM from retrieved evidence are treated as Silver supervision and never as test labels. Because every inference mode reads the same grounded state and emits the same schema, GPS-Bench turns "does multi-agent simulation help?" into a controlled comparison: we contrast joint reasoning, independent and communicating actor agents, graph-based methods and weight-level fine-tuning over one policy state. Fine-tuning on the grounded record gives the strongest actor-level impact prediction, and decomposition does not beat it; what decomposition adds is mechanism. Agents hold private, non-identical evidence, each seeing its own exposure clause, and address named partners with concrete joint proposals, what they offer, what they need in return, and why acting together beats acting alone, so the coalitions that form can be checked against the commitments the record holds. GPS-Bench therefore gives a common empirical setting for studying when evidence, actor modelling and multi-agent interaction improve the prediction and interpretation of policy outcomes.

</details>


### [200] [HalluPeer: A Taxonomy-driven Benchmark for Detecting Hallucinations in Scientific Peer Reviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03580)
*Tzu-Ling Lin,Dong-Ting Yao,Teng-Fang Hsiao,Wei-Chih Chen,Hong-Han Shuai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了HalluPeer，一个用于检测科学同行评审中幻觉的基准测试集，包含论文内容、人工撰写评审和注入幻觉的评审三种对齐三元组，并带有检测、分类和定位标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模学术同行评审促使LLM被用作评审助手，但LLM可能生成流畅但缺乏证据支持的声明，削弱评审可靠性。现有幻觉基准测试并非针对同行评审场景设计，因为同行评审的验证需要将声明与长篇技术论文进行关联。

Method: 构建了一套pipeline：归纳同行评审特定幻觉分类体系、识别评审上下文、注入幻觉并通过自动化过滤筛选。基于12K篇论文和38K条评审进行实验。

Result: 实验表明现有检测器难以区分幻觉与合法批评；在真实评审上的评估显示HalluPeer定义的幻觉模式确实出现在真实同行评审中。

Conclusion: 作者强调需要构建源感知（source-aware）的验证机制来提升LLM评审助手的可靠性。

Abstract: The growing scale of academic peer review has motivated the use of Large Language Models (LLMs) as review assistants, yet LLMs can generate fluent but unsupported claims that undermine review reliability. Existing hallucination benchmarks are not designed for peer review, where verification requires grounding claims in long, technical papers. We introduce HalluPeer, a benchmark for detecting hallucinations in scientific peer reviews, providing aligned triples of paper content, human-written reviews, and hallucination-injected reviews, annotated for detection, classification, and localization. Our pipeline induces a peer-review-specific hallucination taxonomy, identifies review contexts, and injects hallucinations with automated filtering. Experiments on 12K papers and 38K reviews show that existing detectors struggle to separate hallucinations from legitimate critique, while evaluation on authentic reviews demonstrates that HalluPeer-defined hallucination patterns occur in real peer reviews, highlighting the critical need for source-aware verification. Our project page can be found in https://github.com/Lin-TzuLing/HalluPeer.git

</details>


### [201] [The Attention Triangle in Audio-Video Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03586)
*Sagi Polaczek,Noa Kraicer,Gal Metzer,Zhuo Ning,Ali Mahdavi-Amiri,Daniel Cohen-Or,Raja Giryes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究音视频扩散模型中跨模态注意力机制导致的语义泄漏问题，通过分析文本-音频-视频三流之间的"注意力三角"，揭示了音频-视频边缘的语义双向路由及其受模型参数偏差驱动的影响，并提出基于注意力信号的诊断工具和推理时干预方法来改善跨模态语义对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音视频扩散模型依赖跨模态注意力协调文本、声音和视觉内容，但这种机制会引入微妙且系统性的语义泄漏。作者希望理解语义信息如何跨模态路由，以及这种路由如何导致生成结果中的语义工件。

Method: 1) 构建"注意力三角"框架，分析文本、音频、视频三个流之间的三条跨注意力边；2) 探测音频-视频边缘的双向路由特性及其受参数偏差影响的情况；3) 提取注意力衍生信号作为诊断工具，分析语义跨模态分布和锚定；4) 利用这些信号指导推理时的干预，改善跨模态对齐。

Result: 实验表明：音频-视频注意力边缘确实存在双向语义路由；该边缘受模型参数偏差塑造，是语义泄漏的主要来源；当提示与学习先验冲突时，跨模态交互会覆盖预期条件导致语义偏差；注意力衍生信号可用于受控条件下分析和诱发泄漏；推理时干预可改善语义锚定并保持生成质量。

Conclusion: 语义工件不仅源于注意力扩散到非目标区域，还源于沿特定路径的结构化、偏置驱动的交互。通过理解这些机制并提出注意力信号引导的干预方法，可以在保持生成质量的同时实现更一致的跨模态语义对齐。

Abstract: Audio-video diffusion models rely on cross-modal attention to coordinate text, sound, and visual content, yet this same mechanism can introduce subtle and systematic semantic leakage. We study these models by probing and analyzing the ``attention triangle,'' comprising the three cross-attention edges connecting the text, audio, and video streams, and examine how semantic information is routed across modalities during generation. Our analysis reveals that routing along the audio-video edge is bidirectional: audio can influence video generation, while video can influence audio generation. This edge is shaped by biases encoded in the model's parameters and emerges as a major contributor to leakage: when prompts are in tension with learned priors, cross-modal interactions may override the intended conditioning and reroute semantics toward visually canonical but incorrect outcomes. These effects suggest that semantic artifacts arise not merely from attention spreading beyond its intended target, but from structured, bias-driven interactions along specific pathways. Building on this perspective, we extract attention-derived signals that expose how semantics are distributed and grounded across modalities, and use them as a diagnostic tool to both analyze and deliberately incur leakage under controlled conditions. This enables us to probe the internal dynamics of cross-modal routing and isolate the role of individual interactions. We further leverage these signals to guide inference-time interventions that encourage more consistent cross-modal alignment. Extensive experiments support our analysis and demonstrate improved semantic grounding while preserving generation quality.

</details>


### [202] [KC-Bench: A Dynamic Interactive Benchmark for Evaluating Knowledge Conflicts in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03588)
*Yaxing Lyu,Shengjie Zhou,Binbin Toh,Pengyu Zhu,Lijun Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了KC-Bench，一个受控多轮对话基准，用于衡量大语言模型在工具调用场景中处理多种冲突（世界知识冲突、输入不一致、多源时间冲突）的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型越来越多地通过工具执行任务，它们需要协调用户指令、参数化知识和动态环境观察之间的冲突。然而现有基准缺乏对这种冲突感知能力的系统评估。

Method: KC-Bench包含238个经过人工筛选的任务（从1000多个候选生成），结合用户模拟器、有状态工具、确定性环境断言、开源自然语言评估器和人工轨迹验证，对9个模型进行多维度评估。

Result: 评估显示9个模型（包括DeepSeek-V4-Flash、GLM-5.2和MiniMax-M3等）在不同领域表现差异显著，没有模型能在所有设置中可靠处理事实修正、身份一致性检查和时间冲突解决；漏检的冲突可传播到工具调用或合成受保护数据流中。

Conclusion: KC-Bench隔离了模型层面的冲突处理行为而非评估完整代理框架，为开发冲突感知推理和执行安全保障提供了可复现的诊断工具。

Abstract: As LLMs increasingly act through tools, they must reconcile user instructions, parametric knowledge, and dynamic environmental observations before taking actions. We introduce KC-Bench, a controlled multi-turn benchmark for measuring this capability across world-knowledge conflicts, input inconsistencies, and multi-source temporal conflicts. Its 238 tasks are manually screened from more than 1,000 generated candidates and combine a user simulator, stateful tools, deterministic environment assertions, an open-source natural-language evaluator, and human trajectory verification. Evaluation of nine models, including DeepSeek-V4-Flash, GLM-5.2, and MiniMax-M3, shows substantial cross-domain variation: no model handles factual correction, identity consistency checking, and temporal conflict resolution reliably across all settings. In the simulated environments, missed conflicts can propagate to tool calls or synthetic protected-data flows. KC-Bench isolates this model-level behavior rather than ranking complete agent frameworks, and provides a reproducible diagnostic for developing conflict-aware reasoning and execution safeguards.

</details>


### [203] [A computable representation of the physical laboratory enables verifiable workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03621)
*Xiaobo Li,Luyao Ge,Xiaohui Li,Lulu Guo,Ming Mao,Jiwang Zheng,Wenting Guan,Xin Yang,Yi Luo,Jun Jiang,Linjiang Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将科学实验室物理世界计算化的表示方法，通过类型化研究对象、能力受限操作和组合工作流代数，将实验室状态与机器可读知识连接起来，并在模块化智能体机器人实验室中实现，支持从智能体推理到物理操作的自动科学发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 使科学可计算需要同时表示科学知识和物理世界，但目前缺乏一种将物理实验室状态与机器可读知识相结合的计算表示方法。

Method: 通过类型化研究对象、能力受限操作和组合工作流代数建立物理实验室的计算表示，将工作流表示为对演化实验室状态的程序，并绑定可执行的Function Skills实现模块化智能体机器人实验室。

Result: 针对多样科学意图生成了能力相对的工作流，状态模拟传播对象转换并验证操作前提和实验室约束，建立了智能体推理与能力受限物理变换之间的通用计算接口。

Conclusion: 该表示及其工程框架为端到端自主科学发现提供了基础。

Abstract: Making science computable requires representations of both scientific knowledge and the physical world in which scientific claims are tested. A computable representation of the physical laboratory is established through typed research objects, capability-bound operations and a compositional workflow algebra. It provides the physical-world counterpart to machine-readable knowledge, expressing workflows as programs over evolving laboratory states with explicit dependencies, decisions, iteration and concurrency. The representation was implemented in a modular agentic robotic laboratory by binding formal operations to executable Function Skills. For diverse scientific intents, capability-relative workflows were generated, while stateful simulation propagated object transformations and verified operation preconditions and laboratory constraints before dispatch. The proposed representation and its engineering framework jointly establish a general computational interface between agent reasoning and capability-bound physical transformations, providing a foundation for end-to-end autonomous scientific discovery.

</details>


### [204] [Analysis of Prompt Engineering for Drug Toxicity Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03635)
*Mia MacGregor,Aakash Welgamage Don,Mark Bartlett*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了提示工程（Prompt Engineering）在药物毒性预测中的重要性，发现LLM的自然变异对结果的影响超过了提示微调，且使用化学信息学代码提取特征比LLM生成特征能显著提升模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床试验成本高昂且药物失败率约90%，毒性是主要原因。虽然LLM已被广泛用于药物毒性预测，但其对提示措辞高度敏感，这引发了对提示工程可靠性的担忧，需要系统性的分析方法来评估提示设计对预测结果的影响。

Method: 提出了一个分析提示工程的系统方法：1) 构建多种提示变体，涵盖职位角色、提示结构和规则解释三个维度；2) 使用LLM识别与药物毒性预测相关的化学特性；3) 利用LLM生成特征数据集并传递给传统机器学习算法；4) 比较LLM生成特征与化学信息学代码提取特征的性能差异。

Result: 实验表明LLM固有的自然变异对预测结果的影响超过了提示微调带来的提升；但使用化学信息学代码提取特征时，模型性能有显著改善，优于使用LLM生成的特征值。

Conclusion: 提出的分析方法可适用于生物信息学中广泛的提示类型，且提示工程的精细调整效果有限，采用化学信息学工具提取特征更为可靠。

Abstract: Clinical trials in the UK can cost up to £1.3 million, with approximately 90% drug failure rate. Toxicity is a major contributing factor in drug failure. Testing is time and cost intensive. In recent years, the use of artificial intelligence has been increasingly explored to aid in the prediction of drug toxicity, with extensive use of large language models (LLMs). However, LLMs can show considerable variation when minor changes are made to prompts, which raises concerns about their sensitivity to prompt engineering. Prompt engineering is used to optimise a prompt given to an LLM to generate the desired output. This paper proposes a method to analyse prompt engineering for drug toxicity prediction. The aim of the paper is to investigate the importance of prompt phrasing for drug toxicity prediction. LLMs were prompted to identify chemical properties of significance when predicting drug toxicity. Prompts were constructed to investigate; job role, prompt structuring, and rule interpretation. LLMs were then used to generate datasets, using the identified features from initial prompting, which were then passed to machine learning algorithms. The experiments show that the natural variance which occurs in LLMs outweighs any fine-tuning of prompts. There were, however, substantial improvements in model performance when using chemoinformatic code to extract features instead of using LLM-generated values. The proposed analysis methodology is applicable to a wide range of prompt types across different areas of bioinformatics.

</details>


### [205] [Synthetic Semantic Supervision for Contrastive Code Representation Learning in Small Transformers: An Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03702)
*Kenneth Paulsen,Florian Tambon,Mike Papadakis,Shin Yoo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了使用合成生成的自然语言描述（强调代码功能与意图）作为对比预训练监督信号来训练小型代码编码器的方法，在推理时丢弃这些描述。实验表明该方法在八个检索、分类和生成任务上的表现优于相同规模的预训练基线，微调后可与大两个数量级的零样本模型媲美。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用代码嵌入需要高效的编码器，但现有方法依赖人工编写的docstrings（劳动密集且不一致）或采集执行轨迹等结构化信号（依赖特定环境且收集成本高），亟需一种可扩展且有效的替代监督信号。

Method: 提出基于对比预训练的小型编码器方法：利用合成生成的自然语言描述（强调代码功能与意图）与代码配对，采用双编码器框架进行训练，推理时丢弃这些描述。在C、C++和Java上对八个检索、分类和生成任务进行基准测试。

Result: 合成语义监督在八个任务中的五个上相比相同推理规模大小的预训练基线获得统计显著的提升，另外两个任务达到持平；微调后在分类任务上匹配或超越两个数量级更大的零样本模型；在与执行感知监督匹配预训练数据时保持同等水平。

Conclusion: 合成语义监督是现有代码表示范式的可扩展且有效的替代方案，能够在避免高成本人工标注和依赖特定环境信号的同时达到与执行感知监督相当的性能。

Abstract: General-purpose code embeddings power tools for code search, classification, and retrieval. Compact transformer encoders for code typically rely on either human-written docstrings (labor-intensive and inconsistent) or mined structural signals such as execution traces (setting-specific and costly to collect). We empirically study an alternative: contrastive pretraining of small encoders with synthetically generated natural-language descriptions emphasizing code functionality and intent, paired with code in a dual-encoder framework at training and discarded at inference. We benchmark this approach against pretraining-based baselines, generalist LLMs, and embedding-specific models on eight retrieval, classification, and generation tasks across C, C++, and Java. Synthetic semantic supervision yields statistically significant gains over pretraining baselines of the same inference-time size on five of eight tasks, with parity on two more; once fine-tuned, it matches or exceeds zero-shot models two orders of magnitude larger on classification, and it stays on par with execution-aware supervision at matched pretraining data, suggesting a scalable, effective alternative to existing code-representation paradigms.

</details>


### [206] [Counterfactual Routing Using Integer Programming with Constraint Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03707)
*Daniël Vos,Sterre Lutz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文介绍了作者团队在IJCAI 2025反事实路由竞赛中的参赛方案，通过整数规划迭代添加约束的方法，为最短路径问题寻找反事实解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反事实解释可以帮助用户理解为什么某个路线不是最优的，例如"如果某条路不是自行车道，你的路线就会是最优的"。该竞赛旨在为最短路径问题寻找最小的路网修改，使选定路线成为最优路线。

Method: 将问题建模为整数规划（Integer Program），通过迭代地纳入约束条件，直到找到精确解。

Result: 在最终评估中，该方法在解决方案质量上排名第四，但在每个实例上求解速度最快，平均运行时间为9.0秒，而下一快方案的运行时间平均为118.8秒。

Conclusion: 该方法在速度上具有显著优势，但在解质量上仍有提升空间，整体上是一种高效的精确求解方案。

Abstract: We present our submission to the IJCAI 2025 'Counterfactual Routing Competition' (CRC 25). The goal of the competition is to find counterfactual explanations for the shortest path problem. This requires deciding what the minimal changes to a road network would make a route chosen by the user the optimal route. This enables explanations such as "Your suggested route would indeed have been optimal, if road X were not a bicycle path." Our solution models the problem as an integer program, iteratively incorporating constraints until an exact solution is found. In the final evaluation on held-out test instances, our method ranked fourth in solution quality and obtained its solution fastest on every instance, with an average runtime of 9.0 seconds compared to 118.8 seconds for the next-fastest submission.

</details>


### [207] [Artificial Intelligence for Energy Optimization in Data Centers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03716)
*Mohammed Basharath Ullah,Summaiya Unnisa Begum,Mohammed Nadeem Ullah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对约194篇关于AI优化数据中心的文献进行系统筛选和编码分析（63篇），发现该领域存在控制研究与可持续性研究脱节的问题：大多数控制研究仅通过仿真验证，未考虑水消耗和隐含碳，且不同技术族的节能效果区间几乎完全重叠，无法比较优劣。作者提出CLEAR-DC框架，将控制策略分支与工作负载需求分支通过弹性项耦合，并输出涵盖能源、碳、水、隐含碳占比及验证场所的模式化记录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据中心既被AI优化又因AI负载增加，然而现有文献将这两个问题割裂处理：控制研究将工作负载视为外生到达过程，可持续性研究将基础设施视为固定乘数。这种脱节导致AI对数据中心的净效益无法被准确评估。

Method: 采用系统性文献综述方法：通过文档化协议检索约194篇论文，编码其中63篇，分控制导向和可持续性导向两类进行分析。基于编码结果识别十个复现性差距，从后果严重性和可解决性两个维度评分，进而提出CLEAR-DC框架。

Result: 28项控制导向研究中仅5项达到物理硬件或生产设施验证，18项仅限仿真；没有一项考虑水取用或隐含碳。四种技术族的节能区间几乎完全重叠，表明领域无法排序自身方法。代码表、统计数据和产物已在GitHub公开。

Conclusion: AI优化数据中心的文献存在方法论上的根本缺陷：缺乏对净效益的考量和对基础设施全生命周期影响的覆盖。CLEAR-DC框架作为架构和方法论提案，通过显式弹性项和标准化报告模式，为未来研究提供更全面评估AI负荷-优化耦合效应的方法路径。

Abstract: Data centers are increasingly optimized by artificial intelligence and, at the same time, increasingly loaded by it. The literature treats these as two unrelated problems: control studies model workload as an exogenous arrival process, while sustainability studies model infrastructure as a fixed multiplier. We screen roughly 194 papers retrieved through a documented protocol, code 63 of them, and report what the coding shows. Of 28 primary control-oriented studies, 18 are validated in simulation alone and 5 reach physical hardware or a production facility; none account for water withdrawal, and none account for embodied carbon. Reported savings intervals across four technique families overlap almost completely, which means the field cannot presently rank its own methods. Ten recurring gaps are scored for consequence and tractability, and we set out CLEAR-DC, a framework coupling a control-policy branch to a workload-demand branch through an explicit elasticity term, reads out net rather than direct benefit, and emits a schema-conformant record covering energy, carbon, water, embodied share and validation venue. The framework is an architectural and methodological proposal, not a trained system; the contribution we defend empirically is the corpus analysis and the reporting schema derived from it. Coding sheet, derived statistics and all result artifacts: https://github.com/Kimalice/AI-for-Energy-Optimization-in-Data-Centers-Closing-the-Optimizer-Load-Loop

</details>


### [208] [Proactive Service Agents: A Unified Decision Framework, Methods, and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03727)
*Yan Tang,Tingyu Cao,Yuanbo Tang,Huaze Tang,Keer Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对大型语言模型智能体的"主动服务"（Proactive Service）能力进行了系统性综述，给出了以"主动性"为核心的可操作定义，将问题形式化为受授权和风险约束的部分可观测序贯决策过程，并构建了从状态估计到反馈适应的统一决策流水线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体大多将显式用户指令作为固定起点，缺乏主动推断服务机会并决策是否干预的能力。主动服务将决策上移，需要在不确定信息下权衡沉默、询问、协助和行动等选项，但缺乏统一的定义、形式化框架和方法论总结。

Method: 论文首先给出以主动性为核心的操作性定义，将主动服务形式化为受授权和风险约束的部分可观测序贯决策过程，在单一结构化动作中统一表达时机、内容和交付方式。随后沿"状态与需求估计、干预门控、动作构建、反馈适应"一条决策流水线组织现有方法，并将规定式、预测式、基于模型和回报优化机制归类为非互斥的策略构建组件。论文还统一了多种资源（流式对话、屏幕、视频、软件工程、人机协作）的决策单元和三轴证据描述子，并形式化了触发、时机、校准、用户负担、安全性和策略价值等指标。

Result: 论文揭示了离线分类性能不能单独预测部署收益，长期记忆也不是主动性的必要条件。可靠的主动服务需要：校准的增量干预价值、可验证的授权、可恢复的执行以及反事实证据。

Conclusion: 要构建值得信赖的主动服务系统，必须超越离线分类能力，转向对增量干预价值、授权验证、错误可恢复性和反事实评估的严格刻画。

Abstract: Large language model agents can plan, invoke tools, and modify external states, yet most systems still take an explicit user instruction as a fixed starting point. Proactive service moves the decision upstream: an agent must infer service opportunities from incomplete environmental and user signals, choose among remaining silent, asking, assisting, and acting, and account for interruption, misunderstanding, overreach, and privacy costs. This survey gives an operational definition centered on initiative and formulates the problem as a partially observable sequential decision process constrained by authorization and risk. The formulation represents timing, content, and delivery within one structured action, while making explicit the option value of waiting, the decision value of questions, and feedback-induced state changes. On this basis, we organize existing methods along one decision pipeline (state and need estimation, intervention gating, action construction, and feedback adaptation) and describe prescribed, predictive, model based, and return optimizing mechanisms as nonexclusive policy-construction components. We further normalize decision units and three-axis evidence descriptors across streaming dialogue, screen, video, software-engineering, and human-agent collaboration resources, and formalize metrics for triggering, timing, calibration, user burden, safety, and policy value. The synthesis shows why offline classification performance alone does not predict deployment benefit and why long-term memory is not a defining condition of proactivity. Reliable proactive service instead requires calibrated incremental intervention value, verifiable authorization, recoverable execution, and counterfactual evidence.

</details>


### [209] [Rethinking World Models for Safety-Critical Embodied Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03774)
*Kailang Ma,Heye Huang,Inhi Kim,Kitae Jang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"风险知情世界模型"(RIWM)这一决策导向的研究方向，指出当前世界模型在安全关键具身系统上存在三个结构性错配：似然与风险、预测与干预、有限时域预测与累积后果。RIWM围绕后果、干预、认知不确定性、可恢复性组织世界建模，并整合决策相关表征、反事实推理、安全关键情景记忆与运行时安全保证四种能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管世界模型已演变为具身环境的生成式、可控、交互式模拟器，但高预测似然和视觉保真度并不能确保模型保留安全决策所需的证据。论文试图指出现有世界模型的根本缺陷并提出新的研究方向。

Method: 这是一篇perspective（观点性）论文，通过识别当前世界建模中的三个结构性错配（似然vs风险、预测vs干预、有限时域vs累积后果），提出RIWM框架，区分物理、社会与操作后果，利用认知不确定性限定支持行动的证据，并讨论开放挑战。

Result: 论文提出RIWM作为安全关键具身系统的决策中心研究方向，定义了四种相互依赖的能力架构，并识别了六个开放挑战：识别后果性未来、验证反事实推理、维护可修正的安全记忆、将学习到的后果转化为可执行约束、判断证据何时充分到可以行动。

Conclusion: 未来的世界模型应超越预测"可能的未来"，转向识别"哪些未来重要"，通过经验修正判断，并识别何时行动、修正、感知、延迟或放弃。

Abstract: World models have progressed from compact latent dynamics to generative, controllable, and interactive simulators of embodied environments. However, high predictive likelihood and visual fidelity do not necessarily ensure that a model preserves the evidence required for safe decision-making. This perspective identifies three structural mismatches in current world modeling: likelihood versus risk, prediction versus intervention, and finite-horizon prediction versus accumulated consequences. We propose the Risk-Informed World Model (RIWM) as a decision-centric research direction for safety-critical embodied systems. RIWM organizes world modeling around consequences, intervention, epistemic uncertainty, and recoverability, and integrates four interdependent capabilities: decision-relevant representation, counterfactual reasoning, safety-critical episodic memory, and runtime safety assurance. It distinguishes physical, social, and operational consequences while using epistemic uncertainty to qualify the evidence supporting action. We further discuss open challenges in identifying consequential futures, validating counterfactual reasoning, maintaining revisable safety memories, translating learned consequences into executable constraints, and determining when evidence is sufficient to act. This perspective argues that future world models should move beyond predicting likely futures toward identifying which futures matter, revising judgments through experience, and recognizing when to act, revise, sense, defer, or abstain.

</details>


### [210] [DNative-Twin: Decision Graphs and Digital Twins for Reconstructable Agentic Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03787)
*Junjie Pang,Zhenzhen Xie,Haoke Han,Ying He,Jing Wang,Gang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DNative-Twin，一个图原生数字孪生框架，用于记录和重放智能体决策过程，将决策审计从纯结果回溯推进到机制层面的可验证重放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体越来越多地收集证据、调用工具、应用约束并产生决策，但最终输出无法显示产生该决策的证据、工具状态、规则、授权或行动路径，导致决策可审计性不足。

Method: 提出DNative-Twin框架，以图原生数字孪生的方式将智能体决策记录为类型化轨迹，建立状态-路径-权威之间的图关联，并在隔离环境中重放决策机制，在受控条件下进行比较分析。

Result: 在三个公开流程日志上实验，识别出图结构局限性的具体失败模式；加入重放契约状态后未解决分歧召回率从0提升至0.667，加入验证结果后提升至1.0；端到端时间在500-5000个BPI 2020案例下从0.794秒增至8.889秒。

Conclusion: 该工作区分了图结构、重放上下文和验证证据在决策机制审查中的不同作用，为智能体决策的可审计性提供了一种系统化框架。

Abstract: AI agents increasingly gather evidence, invoke tools, apply constraints, and produce decisions that people or software may commit to action. A final output alone cannot show which evidence, tool state, rule, authorization, or action path produced it. We present DNative-Twin, a graph-native digital twin that records a committed agentic decision as a typed trajectory and re-executes its decision mechanism under declared conditions. The graph links the state observed by the agent, the path it followed, and the authority behind the resulting action. The twin synchronizes this information, replays the mechanism in isolation, and compares it under controlled changes.
