We propose XCal-FL, a closed-loop, explainability-driven local training algorithm for image classification in cross-silo FL that dynamically calibrates DP noise from three complementary signals: (1) prediction logit variations, measuring causal influence on model confidence, (2) counterfactual margins, capturing decision-boundary sensitivity, and (3) saliency concentration, quantifying spatial coherence of model attention, while enforcing formal DP guarantees via adaptive privacy accounting. Experiments on three medical imaging datasets across varying FL configurations show that XCal-FL yields more accurate and interpretable global models, improving predictive performance by over 10\% and explanation fidelity by up to 5$\times$ over static-noise FL, and outperforming state-of-the-art adaptive DP methods in fidelity. XCal-FL also achieves higher privacy-budget efficiency, turning each unit of cumulative privacy loss into larger gains in both accuracy and explanation fidelity. Our analysis further reveals that, unlike predictive performance, which scales roughly linearly with privacy loss, explanation fidelity exhibits non-linear dynamics. These findings suggest explainability is a distinct dimension of the privacy trade-off that cannot be inferred from utility alone, with implications for training and privacy-budget allocation in decision-critical applications.

</details>


### [316] [High-Dimensional Learning Dynamics of Attention-Indexed Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03858)
*Yizhou Xu,Margarita Sagitova,Lenka Zdeborová,Florent Krzakala*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了注意力机制的训练动态，特别是当注意力矩阵具有高秩时的随机梯度下降（SGD）行为。作者提出attention-indexed模型框架，在高维极限下用有限阶参数刻画种群损失景观，发现注意力参数化本身可作为架构性隐式偏差，并揭示了tied与untied注意力在不同样本复杂度下的对称性破缺机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 注意力机制是现代基础模型的核心，但其训练动态——尤其是注意力矩阵具有高秩时的训练行为——仍未被充分理解。理解这些动态有助于揭示注意力架构的隐式偏差和样本效率特点。

Method: 提出attention-indexed模型框架，可表示多层和多头注意力架构。在高维极限下用有限trace序参数刻画种群损失景观，并用有限截断系统指数级逼近在线SGD的无限矩阵矩层级。

Result: （1）注意力参数化本身可作为架构性隐式偏差；（2）直接优化注意力矩阵S可能陷入无信息状态；（3）Tied注意力（S=WW^T）在Θ(d²log d)样本下通过自动对称性破缺实现弱恢复；（4）Untied注意力（S=UV^T）存在快慢机制，快动力学选择的态打破初始对称性时，在Θ(d²log d)尺度实现弱恢复。

Conclusion: 注意力参数化方式（tied vs untied）对训练动态和样本复杂度有本质影响，框架揭示了注意力架构中隐式偏差的来源，为理解注意力机制的训练行为提供了理论工具。

Abstract: Attention mechanisms are central to modern foundation models, yet their training dynamics remain poorly understood, especially when the attention matrices have extensive rank. In this work, we study attention-indexed models, a broad framework that can represent multi-layer and multi-head attention architectures. First, we show that, in a suitable high-dimensional limit, the population-loss landscape is characterized by a finite set of trace order parameters. In contrast, online stochastic gradient descent (SGD) is governed by an infinite hierarchy of matrix moments, which we show can be exponentially well-approximated by a finite truncated system. Second, this framework reveals that attention parameterization itself can act as an architectural implicit bias. Direct optimization of an attention matrix $S\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times d}$ can remain trapped in an uninformative state. Tied attention ($S=WW^\top$) induces an automatic symmetry-breaking mechanism and yields weak recovery in $Θ(d^2\log d)$ samples. For untied attention, $S=UV^\top$, we uncover a fast-slow mechanism: the pre-activation mean first evolves on a fast timescale, while the overlaps evolve on a slower one. Weak recovery on the $Θ(d^2\log d)$ scale occurs when the state selected by the fast dynamics breaks the initial symmetry.

</details>


### [317] [Differentiable Interval Bottlenecks for Interpretable Anomaly Detection in Numerical Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03878)
*Lamine Diop,Marc Plantevit*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DIFFINT，一种具有可解释瓶颈结构的自编码器异常检测方法，通过端到端学习软轴对齐区间隶属度（无需离散化），每个隐单元对应特征空间中的可读超矩形，在48个ADBench基准上达到最优平均排名，且是唯一进入统计显著领先集群的可解释检测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于重建的异常检测器（如深度自编码器）虽然准确但缺乏可解释性——系统能标记异常样本，却无法告诉从业者哪些特征范围导致了异常。现有方法缺少在保持深度学习表示能力的同时提供可检查内部结构的方案。

Method: DIFFINT将自编码器的潜在瓶颈结构化为一组软的、轴对齐的区间隶属度，端到端从原始数值数据中直接学习，无需离散化或二值化。每个隐单元对应特征空间中的一个人类可读超矩形；实例的编码表示其落入每个区间的强度，重建误差作为异常分数。论文还提供了：通过Lipschitz约束解码器保证的外部点重建误差下界、分级抑制机制，以及无需标签的闭合形式重要性度量来排名(单元,特征)对。

Result: 在48个ADBench基准上对比22个基线，DIFFINT在两个指标上均取得最优平均排名（ROC-AUC为4.10，AUPR为4.16）；在仅用正常数据训练的检测器中明显领先，与最强者污染数据的检测器竞争激烈；且是七个统计并列领先方法中唯一的可解释检测器。

Conclusion: DIFFINT证明可解释性和检测性能可以兼得，通过结构化的可读瓶颈设计，在不牺牲准确性的前提下提供了可审计的异常约束候选，填补了可解释深度学习异常检测的重要空白。

Abstract: Reconstruction-based anomaly detectors are accurate but opaque: a deep autoencoder flags a sample without telling a practitioner which feature ranges made it anomalous. We propose DIFFINT, an autoencoder whose latent bottleneck is structured as a set of soft, axis-aligned interval memberships learned end-to-end directly from raw numerical data, without any discretization or binarization. Each latent unit corresponds to a human-readable hyper-rectangle in feature space; an instance is encoded by how strongly it falls inside each interval relative to the other units, and its reconstruction error is the anomaly score. This keeps the power of differentiable representation learning while exposing an inspectable internal structure. We make the inductive bias precise: a certified reconstruction-error lower bound for points that fall outside every active coordinate of the learned support (with a Lipschitz-enforced decoder), and a graded, empirically verified suppression mechanism for the usual case in which only a few features are abnormal; and we provide a closed-form, label-free importance that ranks each (unit, feature) pair from quantities the model already maintains, turning trained intervals into auditable candidate constraints without ever seeing an anomaly label. On 48 ADBench benchmarks against 22 baselines under a common [-1, 1]-normalized protocol, DIFFINT attains the best mean rank overall on both metrics (4.10 on ROC-AUC, 4.16 on AUPR); among inlier-only detectors it leads its regime clearly, and it is competitive with the strongest contaminated-data detectors (see the stratified and complete-case analyses). It is the only interpretable detector in the statistically-tied leading cluster of seven methods.

</details>


### [318] [Beyond Endpoint Scores: Time- and Capacity-Conditioned Evaluation of Continual Knowledge Updating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03900)
*Heejin Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现持续知识更新方法的优劣比较会随评估时间、回放LoRA秩和查询表述方式而变化，单一检查点和单一适配器秩的比较不足以确定更优的操作点，因此提出报告轨迹和容量扫描的建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续知识更新方法常仅从单一最终检查点和单一适配器秩来宣称优越性，但这种评估方式可能不足以识别真正更好的操作点，方法排名可能随评估条件变化而变化。

Method: 在24个月的Wikidata数据流上，将周期性分层方法与累积回放方法进行比较，系统性地变化评估月份、回放LoRA秩和查询表述方式，并使用Qwen2.5-1.5B、Llama-3.2-1B以及保留的改写数据集进行验证。

Result: 在不同评估条件下，胜负方会反转：在Qwen2.5-1.5B上，分层方法对秩8回放有5.0点优势，但对秩72回放反而落后11.6点；在高层级上，整合对齐端点看似平局，但时间平均回放领先9-13点。同样的秩条件反转现象在Llama-3.2-1B和保留改写集上也出现。

Conclusion: 持续更新中的方法排名取决于评估时间和回放侧适配容量两个因素。应报告轨迹和容量扫描，仅在排序跨评估区域稳定时才宣称稳健的胜者，否则应报告胜者区域和保持性-稳定性-成本边界。在此协议下，周期性分层应被视为低更新成本的操作点，而非质量上的胜者。

Abstract: Continual knowledge-updating methods are often declared superior from one final checkpoint and one conventional adapter rank. We show that this can be insufficient to identify the better operating point. Holding a periodic hierarchy fixed, we compare it with cumulative replay over a 24-month Wikidata stream while varying evaluation month, replay LoRA rank, and query formulation. The apparent winner changes across this region: on Qwen2.5-1.5B, the hierarchy's 5.0-point advantage over rank-8 replay becomes an 11.6-point deficit against rank-72 replay, and at high ranks a consolidation-aligned endpoint can suggest a tie while time-averaged replay leads by 9-13 points. The same rank-conditioned reversal appears on Llama-3.2-1B and held-out paraphrases.
  These results show that method ranking in continual updating can depend jointly on when performance is measured and how much replay-side adaptation capacity the baseline receives. We therefore propose reporting trajectories and capacity sweeps, and declaring a robust winner only when the ordering is stable across the evaluation region; otherwise, comparisons should report winner regions and retention-stability-cost frontiers. Under this protocol, the periodic hierarchy is a lower-update-cost operating point, not a quality winner.

</details>


### [319] [RATL: Learning from Retrieved Residuals for Robust Multivariate Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03937)
*Yuchen He,Yueyang Cang,Zhiyuan Ning,Ningyu Wang,Li Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RATL是一种用于多元时间序列预测的即插即用残差检索与反馈修正方法，冻结基础预测器，将历史预测残差作为可训练记忆，在推断时检索相似历史残差轨迹并进行路由组合，以改进冻结基础预测器的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG方法在文本生成中有效，但直接复用检索到的目标值对连续输出回归不稳健；传统预测管线仅用残差做优化和诊断，未将历史残差作为可访问的记忆用于推断。

Method: RATL冻结基础预测器构建检索键，将历史预测残差转化为该基础模型专属的可训练记忆；推断时考虑因果可用性约束检索相似历史残差轨迹，并通过面向预测块和变量的集合感知路由器选择并组合这些轨迹。

Result: 实验表明匹配上下文的历史残差包含可复用的预测信息，RATL在大多数实验设置中改进冻结基础预测器；消融研究显示学习路由增强原始残差反馈，验证集上选择修正强度可限制残差过度注入；以iTransformer为主冻结基座在真实基准上对比多个强基线，验证了跨骨干的可迁移性。

Conclusion: RATL将检索对象从历史目标值转变为基座模型特有的历史预测误差，为连续输出预测提供了基于残差记忆的即插即用学习反馈修正范式。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) complements parametric models with retrieved external evidence. The same idea is attractive for continuous-output regression, but directly reusing retrieved target values is often not robust when samples differ in output level, numerical scale, or local dynamics. Moreover, conventional forecasting pipelines generally use residuals for model optimization and error diagnosis, but do not retain individual historical residual examples as memory that can be accessed at inference time.For multivariate time-series forecasting, we propose RATL, a plug-in residual-retrieval and feedback-correction method. RATL freezes a base forecaster to construct retrieval keys and turns its historical forecast residuals into a train-only memory specific to that base model. At inference time, RATL retrieves residual trajectories from similar historical contexts subject to causal availability constraints, then uses a set-aware router operating over forecast blocks and variables to select and combine these trajectories. Experiments show that historical residuals matched to the current context contain reusable forecasting information and that RATL improves frozen base forecasters in most experimental settings. Ablations further show that learned routing strengthens raw residual feedback, while validation-based correction-strength selection limits residual over-injection.On real-world benchmarks, we use iTransformer as the primary frozen base forecaster, compare against multiple strong forecasting baselines, and test transferability across backbones. The results show that RATL can further improve base-forecaster performance in most settings.Overall, RATL shifts the retrieved object from historical target values to base-model-specific historical forecast errors, providing a plug-in, residual-memory-based paradigm for learned feedback correction in continuous-output forecasting.

</details>


### [320] [Headroom-Drift Replay: A Primitive for Principled Replay Control in GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03941)
*Hyun Bin Park,Du-Seong Chang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Headroom-Drift Replay，一种用于GRPO的组级重放控制原语，将重放选择分解为Headroom（按剩余学习价值排序）和Drift（按与当前策略的兼容性筛选）两个决策，在不改变新鲜on-policy数据流的情况下显著提升重放效率，在数学推理、多模态推理和Agentic Search基准上均优于朴素重放并达到或超过更复杂的重放方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于RL的后训练中，推理模型因反复生成新rollout而面临计算瓶颈，尤其在agentic场景中环境交互主导墙钟时间。Replay可通过重用历史轨迹减轻负担，但现有方法通常将重放嵌入包含探索、经验重构或混合策略优化的复杂训练管线中，难以隔离重放本身的作用。作者希望回答一个聚焦问题：仅靠原则性的重放选择能走多远？

Method: 提出Headroom-Drift Replay，一种针对GRPO的组级重放控制原语。它将重用分为两个独立决策：(1) Headroom——对存储的组按剩余学习价值进行排序；(2) Drift——根据与当前策略的兼容性对组进行门控。新鲜on-policy数据流保持完全不变，方法不引入任何辅助生成或额外训练机制。

Result: 在数学推理、多模态推理和Agentic Search基准上，该方法作为单一干预措施优于朴素重放，并在Avg Mean@32指标上达到或超过更全面的重放方法。在Agentic Search中（环境交互主导成本），该方法在显著降低墙钟时间的同时保持了可比质量。

Conclusion: 单一、可正确隔离的重放选择原语——无需额外探索或复杂管线——即可在多种推理场景中达到甚至超越更复杂的重放方法，尤其在环境交互成本高昂的agentic场景中具有显著的效率优势。

Abstract: RL-based post-training for reasoning models is increasingly bottlenecked by repeated fresh rollout generation, particularly in agentic settings where environment interaction dominates wall-clock cost. Replay can reduce this burden by reusing past trajectories, but existing methods typically embed it within larger training pipelines involving exploration, experience restructuring, or mixed-policy optimization. This makes replay's own contribution difficult to isolate. We ask a focused question: how far can principled replay selection alone go? We introduce Headroom-Drift Replay, a group-level replay control primitive for GRPO that separates reuse into two decisions. Headroom ranks stored groups by remaining learning value, while Drift gates them by compatibility with the current policy. The fresh on-policy stream remains unchanged, and the method adds no auxiliary generation or training machinery. Across mathematical reasoning, multimodal reasoning, and Agentic Search benchmarks, this single intervention outperforms naive replay and matches or exceeds broader replay methods on Avg Mean@32. In Agentic Search, where environment interaction dominates cost, it delivers comparable quality at materially lower wall-clock time.

</details>


### [321] [VestigeKV: The NoPE-MLA KV Cache Carries Its Own Eviction Signal in a Vestigial Branch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03949)
*WenJie Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出VestigeKV，一种针对NoPE MLA模型的KV缓存压缩方法，利用decoupled branch作为query-independent的显著性信号，实现无需训练、量化和内核修改的高效缓存淘汰，在8x-32x压缩下保持近乎完美的检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长生命周期的KV缓存必须在查询存在之前进行压缩，而基于观察注意力的方法（如H2O、SnapKV）在NoPE MLA模型上失效（needle retrieval仅0.00-0.33），因为token的重要性在压缩时尚未被观察到。

Method: VestigeKV利用Kimi Linear中64维decoupled branch（NoPE训练将其重塑为salience通道）作为query-independent信号。读取每行11%的数据来划分缓存：top-m行保留在attended层，其余行迁移（而非删除）到GPU常驻的archive中，可通过certified trigger逐步获取。无需训练、量化或权重/内核修改。

Result: 在8x压缩下检索保持1.00，32x下保持0.92（8k到65k上下文），与全行选择零差距。32x时attended层仅占Kimi Linear每token缓存8.1KB中的0.25KB；archive保持bit-exact且GPU常驻。Recall层标准配置在128x下仍保持1.00。该操作在RoPE MLA上崩溃至0.08。

Conclusion: VestigeKV证明在NoPE架构下存在query-independent的显著性信号可用于高效KV缓存压缩，而RoPE下该信号不存在且query-universal精确合并在理论上不可行。该方法可能扩展到使用NoPE Gated-MLA的Kimi K3，但仅在已测模型上做出声明。

Abstract: The problem. A long-lived KV cache must be compressed before the queries that will read it exist; selection by observed attention (H2O, SnapKV) collapses there (0.00-0.33 needle retrieval on a NoPE MLA model), because a token's importance has not yet been observed. The method. On Kimi Linear, VestigeKV evicts by a query-independent signal the cache already carries: the 64-dimensional decoupled branch, a vestige of RoPE that NoPE training repurposes into a salience channel. Reading 11% of each row, it partitions the cache: the top-m rows stay in the attended tier; every other row moves -- exactly, never deleted -- to a GPU-resident archive reachable per step by a certified trigger. No training, no quantization, no weight or kernel change. Cost. Nothing measurable: retrieval holds at 1.00 under 8x and 0.92 under 32x from 8k to 65k context, zero gap to full-row selection. The attended tier is 0.25 KB of Kimi Linear's 8.1 KB per-token cache at 32x; the archive stays bit-exact and GPU-resident, with host offload as the VRAM-reclaiming variant. The recall tier -- the standard configuration -- holds 128x at 1.00. Kimi K3 is reported to use a NoPE Gated-MLA variant; if its cache layout matches, the method plausibly extends there -- we make no claim beyond the measured model. NoPE exclusivity. The identical operator on a RoPE MLA collapses to 0.08 (plain eviction: 0.42); query-independent salience itself exists only without rotation (top-1 targets span 2.3-6.7% of tokens vs. 10.2-46.8%), and query-universal exact merging is provably impossible under RoPE. All thresholds were frozen before data; 20 archived verdicts and 8 closed routes accompany the paper.

</details>


### [322] [OSR: Output Space Redistribution for Adaptive Label Removal in Classification Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03972)
*Minyi Peng,Darian Gunamardi,Ivan Tjuawinata,Yongsen Zheng,Kwok-Yan Lam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于输出空间统计重分布的标签移除方法，作为模块化输出过滤器，无需重新训练或特征空间调整，即可近似模拟重训练模型的置信度输出，兼顾计算效率与隐私保护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分类系统中标签移除频繁发生（如分类体系动态更新），现有方法要么依赖原始数据重新训练，要么进行特征空间调整，普遍存在计算/存储开销大、结果不稳定、可扩展性差及模型效用下降等问题。

Method: 通过在输出空间进行统计重分布，近似重训练模型在标签移除后的置信度向量。该方法作为模块化输出过滤器，避免特征空间调整或损失函数收敛，仅需现有标签和先验输出置信度即可工作。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法在多个分类任务上性能可与完全重训练相媲美，同时在计算效率和隐私保护方面有显著提升。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种轻量级、可扩展且隐私友好的标签移除方案，有望成为现有重训练方案的实用替代。

Abstract: Label removal occurs frequently in classification systems with evolving taxonomies, where categories must be dynamically updated or eliminated. To accommodate such changes, classification models must adapt accordingly. Existing solutions, broadly categorized as retraining-based and feature-space-adjustment-based, share common limitations despite their variations, including reliance on access to original data, substantial computational and storage costs, inconsistent results, poor scalability, and degradation of model utility. To address this, we propose a novel approach that leverages statistical redistribution in the output space to approximate the post-removal confidence vectors of a retrained model. Applicable as a modular output filter, our method bypasses the burden of feature-space adjustments or loss-function convergence, alleviating scalability limitations. Furthermore, by requiring only existing labels and prior output confidences, the method potentially mitigates privacy concerns inherent to data-dependent solutions. Extensive experiments demonstrate competitive performance against full retraining, with improvements in computational efficiency and privacy preservation across several classification tasks.

</details>


### [323] [RobustSeiz: An Open-Source Framework for Benchmarking the Robustness of EEG Seizure Detection Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04007)
*Mohammad Mohammadi,Alireza Zarei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RobustSeiz是一个开源的、模型无关的框架，用于在临床驱动的分布偏移下对癫痫发作检测器进行压力测试和标准化基准评估，覆盖环境、噪声和对抗性扰动等多种场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 癫痫发作检测器在未见数据上表现良好，但在现实世界的采集变异性、伪影和对抗性输入下可能失效，缺乏标准化的部署前鲁棒性评估协议。

Method: 将四个公开头皮脑电数据集（CHB-MIT、TUSZ、Siena、SeizeIT1）标准化为BIDS-EEG格式，在预定义的超参数网格上扫描环境、噪声和对抗性变换，提供样本级和事件级评估指标、蒙特卡洛dropout一致性分析，并集成Docker GPU流水线和实验注册机制。

Result: 在TUSZ数据集上使用当代癫痫检测器演示了完整扰动网格评估；AWGN分析展示了扰动严重程度如何影响检测质量、发作起始时间和预测一致性。

Conclusion: RobustSeiz为癫痫检测器的鲁棒性评估提供了共享基准标准，将部署前评估从仅关注干净数据准确率扩展到现实临床压力场景。

Abstract: Despite strong performance on held-out electroencephalography (EEG) data, seizure detectors may fail under real-world acquisition variability, artifacts, and adversarial inputs. We introduce RobustSeiz, an open-source, model-agnostic framework that provides a standardized, reproducible protocol for stress-testing and comparing seizure detectors under controlled, clinically motivated distribution shifts before deployment. We standardize four public scalp-EEG corpora (CHB-MIT, TUSZ, Siena, and SeizeIT1) into BIDS-EEG trees and evaluate subject-independent detectors on held-out splits. Environment, noise, and adversarial transforms are swept over predefined hyperparameter grids. Each run reports sample- and event-level sensitivity, precision, F1, false positives per 24 h, Lead and Lag onset timing, and Monte Carlo dropout predictive agreement. RobustSeiz includes a Dockerized GPU pipeline, experiment registry, and full-evaluation and research-subset modes. We demonstrate the framework with a contemporary seizure detector on TUSZ across the complete implemented shift grid; an AWGN analysis illustrates how perturbation severity changes detection quality, onset timing, and predictive agreement. RobustSeiz provides a shared benchmarking standard for evaluating seizure-detector robustness under realistic clinical stressors, extending pre-deployment assessment beyond clean-data accuracy.

</details>


### [324] [Unlocking Lossless Speedups in LLMs via Discrete Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04010)
*Subham Sekhar Sahoo,Lingjie Chen,Khiem Pham,Jonathan Geuter,Chaitanya Dwivedi,Varad Pimpalkhute,Yash Akhauri,Alexander Moreno,Mikhail Yurochkin,Zhenting Wang,Mostafa Elhoushi,Nolan Dey,Shane Bergsma,Joel Hestness,John Thickstun,Eric Xing,Zhengzhong Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Uno，一种扩散增强的大型语言模型，通过在标准自回归LLM上附加轻量级扩散权重，实现并行多token生成，在不牺牲AR模型质量的情况下获得高达3倍加速，并优于现有推测解码和扩散LLM方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归LLM虽然依赖next-token prediction获得成功，但其顺序生成方式导致推理速度缓慢，成为生成效率的瓶颈。

Method: 将模型参数解耦为AR权重（用标准NTP目标训练）和轻量扩散权重（通过扩散蒸馏阶段训练，用于并行生成多个token）。同时提出Ψ-Spec采样器族，支持固定上下文长度下的无损加速和推理时扩展。

Result: Uno在各类batch size下吞吐量高于领先的推测解码方法，相比基础AR模型最高获得3倍加速；8B Uno模型在智能体工具使用、编码和长上下文推理的所有评测基准上均优于26B DiffusionGemma和Mercury 2。

Conclusion: Uno代表了一种新范式，可通过从头训练或增强现有开源权重AR模型来构建，兼得AR模型的质量与扩散模型并行生成的效率。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) owe much of their success to next-token prediction (NTP), but their autoregressive (AR) structure requires slow, sequential token generation. To overcome this bottleneck, we introduce diffusion-augmented LLMs, a new class of models that defines an AR model distribution while using diffusion to draw multiple tokens in parallel from that distribution. We decouple the parameters of these models into two sets: AR weights, trained using the standard NTP objective, and lightweight diffusion weights, trained to generate multiple tokens simultaneously. The diffusion weights are learned through a simple Diffusion Distillation phase that adds negligible overhead to existing LLM training pipelines. We also introduce $Ψ$-Spec, a family of samplers that enables lossless acceleration and inference-time scaling at a fixed context length. Unlike speculative decoding, our method requires no separate draft model. Unlike diffusion LLMs (d-LLMs), it accelerates generation without sacrificing the quality of the underlying AR model. The resulting models, called Uno, can be trained from scratch or built by augmenting existing open-weight AR LLMs. Uno achieves higher throughput than leading speculative-decoding methods at every evaluated batch size and delivers up to $3\times$ speedups over the base AR model, including at the largest batch size supported by the device. Notably, our 8B Uno model outperforms the leading open d-LLM, the 26B DiffusionGemma, and the proprietary Mercury 2 across all evaluated benchmarks in agentic tool use, coding, and long-context reasoning. We release code and checkpoints at: https://s-sahoo.github.io/uno/

</details>


### [325] [A location-invariant estimator of extremal quantile treatment effects for heavy-tailed distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04018)
*Xin Yu,Shuwei Huang,Jicheng Liu,Jielin Tang,Bolin Wang,Yunxiao Zhang,Tian Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种具有位置不变性的极端分位数处理效应（QTE）估计方法，通过因果极端值指数估计和差分外推方案，解决了现有估计量在重尾潜在结果分布下对公共位置偏移不敏感的问题，并建立了渐近理论和有效的统计推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在重尾结果分布下估计极端分位数处理效应时，现有估计量依赖于外推与因果极端值指数估计相结合，但该估计量对潜在结果分布的公共位置偏移不具不变性，而总体QTE本身是位置不变的，这导致估计量性质不佳。

Method: 两步法：首先将位置不变的Fraga极端值指数估计器通过逆倾向得分加权适应到因果设定中；其次用差分式外推方案替代原始外推公式，使得分位数差分时位置参数相互抵消，从而实现QTE估计量的位置不变性。

Result: 建立了所提极端QTE估计量的一致性和渐近正态性，提供了相合方差估计量以实现渐近有效的推断。仿真研究表明所提方法具有位置不变性、对阈值选择的稳定性以及良好的覆盖率。

Conclusion: 所提出的位置不变极端QTE估计方法在理论和实证层面均有效，弥补了现有方法在重尾分布下因位置偏移导致估计偏差的不足。

Abstract: Quantile treatment effects (QTEs) measure the effect of a treatment on the distribution of an outcome, and their estimation at extreme quantile levels is of central interest in applications where the target quantiles lie far beyond the range of the data. For heavy-tailed potential outcomes, existing extremal QTE estimators rely on extrapolation combined with a causal extreme value index (EVI) estimator, but the resulting estimator is not invariant under a common location shift of the potential outcome distributions, even though the population QTE is. We address this issue in two steps. First, we adapt the location-invariant Fraga estimator of the EVI to the causal setting using inverse propensity score weighting. Second, we replace the original extrapolation formula with a difference-based scheme, under which the location parameter cancels when quantile differences are taken. The resulting QTE estimator is therefore location invariant. We establish the consistency and asymptotic normality of the proposed extremal QTE estimators, and provide a consistent variance estimator, leading to asymptotically valid inference. A simulation study confirms the location invariance, the stability with respect to the threshold, and the coverage of the proposed methods.

</details>


### [326] [Subspace Inference Enables Efficient Active Reward Learning from Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04066)
*Yutai Zhou,Erdem Bıyık*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PreferenceEKF，一种基于扩展卡尔曼滤波的样本高效主动偏好学习方法，用于RLHF中的奖励模型不确定性追踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLHF中从人类偏好学习奖励模型是样本低效的，主动学习至关重要，但对大型神经网络奖励模型进行有效的不确定性量化是一个关键挑战。

Method: 将主动偏好学习建模为序贯贝叶斯滤波问题，通过在低维参数子空间中使用扩展卡尔曼滤波进行序贯推断，避免了对全网络参数空间进行计算密集的后验推断，可持续更新奖励模型后验并高效采样网络参数以计算采集函数。

Result: 在D4RL和V-D4RL基准上，该方法在样本效率、运行时间、可扩展性和校准方面优于其他贝叶斯深度学习方法，学到的奖励模型带来了有竞争力的离线强化学习策略性能。

Conclusion: 展示了可扩展贝叶斯方法在RLHF偏好奖励建模中的潜力。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) has emerged as a powerful yet sample-inefficient approach for learning reward models from human preferences, making active learning a critical component in synthesizing informative preference queries. However, effective uncertainty quantification required for active learning remains a key challenge for large neural network reward models. In this paper, we introduce PreferenceEKF, a sample-efficient approach that tracks reward model uncertainty by framing active preference learning as a sequential Bayesian filtering problem. Instead of relying on computationally prohibitive posterior inference over the full neural network parameter space, our method performs sequential inference via an extended Kalman filter within a low-dimensional parameter subspace, continuously updating the reward model posterior as new preference queries arrive. Our approach enables scalable sampling of neural network parameters to efficiently compute acquisition functions for active reward learning. Experiments on the D4RL and V-D4RL benchmarks demonstrate that our approach achieves better sample efficiency, runtime, scalability, and calibration compared to other Bayesian deep learning approaches, and the learned reward models lead to competitive offline reinforcement learning policy performance. This highlights the potential of scalable Bayesian methods for preference-based reward modeling in RLHF. Our code is available at https://github.com/yutaizhou/bnn_pref.

</details>


### [327] [Conditioning Degenerate Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04090)
*Uğur Aydın,Tamer Başar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出使用因果最优传输来定义近似的损失函数，用于在扩散系数奇异且条件密度不存在或不光滑的情况下，识别最小熵控制以指导条件生成模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前条件生成模型严重依赖得分函数进行训练指导，但当扩散过程的扩散系数奇异且底层条件密度不存在或不光滑时，传统方法失效，需要新的理论框架。

Method: 利用因果最优传输及其通过条件扩散过程的鞅问题的可预测表示性质（à la Üstünel）的表征，构建近似的损失函数来识别最小熵控制。

Result: 在最小假设下，因果最优传输方法能够有效定义近似损失函数，解决扩散系数奇异且密度不光滑情况下的条件生成问题。

Conclusion: 因果最优传输为条件生成模型在弱正则条件下的指导提供了一个坚实的理论框架。

Abstract: Current conditioned generative models heavily rely on score functions for guidance during training. When the generative model is a diffusion process with a singular diffusion coefficient and the underlying (conditional) densities either do not exist or are not smooth, we use causal optimal transport to define \emph{approximate} loss functions that identify a minimum-entropy control for guidance under minimal assumptions. Our approach relies on causal optimal transport and its characterization through the predictable representation property of (conditioned) diffusion processes whose associated martingale problem is well posed, à la Üstünel.

</details>


### [328] [Hardware-Aware FP4 FlashAttention-4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04105)
*Robert Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Direct-P，一种在4-bit浮点（FP4）张量核心上直接进行注意力计算的方法，通过将前向量化直接传递到反向传播中，实现了非因果推理和因果训练路径的加速。在NVIDIA GB200上，非因果前向吞吐量最高达BF16的2.13倍，8B参数模型的完整单GPU训练更新最高加速1.14倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Blackwell的FP4张量核心并不能自动加速注意力计算，因为softmax转换和片上依赖成为瓶颈，一旦矩阵乘积缩小，这些开销就占据主导地位。

Method: 提出Direct-P用于非因果推理，将得分直接映射为FP4概率，避免softmax转换开销；因果路径则将前向量化信息直接传递到反向传播中，从保存的量化查询和键重建概率，并使用FP8梯度操作数。

Result: 非因果推理正向前向吞吐量在GB200上最高达到BF16的2.13倍；8B参数模型的完整单GPU更新最高加速1.14倍。匹配的分布式训练可保留FP8概率和值，而所有测试的MXFP4概率/值训练轨迹均发散。

Conclusion: Direct-P通过在FP4张量核心上直接进行注意力计算绕过softmax转换瓶颈，并设计因果路径利用量化信息加速反向传播，证明了低精度浮点注意力在推理和训练中的可行性。

Abstract: Blackwell's 4-bit floating-point (FP4) tensor cores do not automatically make attention faster because softmax conversion and on-chip dependencies dominate once its matrix products shrink. We address this with \emph{Direct-P} for noncausal inference and a causal path that passes the forward quantization directly into backward. Direct-P maps scores directly to FP4 probabilities and reaches up to 2.13$\times$ the bfloat16 (BF16) forward throughput on an NVIDIA GB200. The causal path reconstructs probabilities from saved quantized queries and keys and uses 8-bit floating-point (FP8) gradient operands, accelerating a complete single-GPU 8-billion-parameter update by up to 1.14$\times$. Matched distributed training retains FP8 probabilities and values; every tested MXFP4 probability/value training trajectory diverges.

</details>


### [329] [Constant regret in general games via higher-order optimism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04113)
*Omar Abbadi,Rida Laraki,Panayotis Mertikopoulos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种名为HOOD（高阶乐观折扣算法）的非耦合学习算法，用于N人一般正规博弈，实现了O(N³log²K)的个体遗憾保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在一般N人博弈中实现常数遗憾是长期以来的挑战，此前的方法无法有效抑制玩法序列的大幅振荡，阻碍了常数遗憾界的达成。

Method: 提出HOOD算法，它是乐观跟随正则化领导者（OptFTRL）的变体，结合了折扣(N+1)阶预测器与在博弈策略空间"提升"上的熵正则化，通过设计抑制诱导玩法序列的振荡。

Result: 实现了O(N³log²K)的个体遗憾界，在全体博弈参与者的时间跨度上均匀成立，显著优于先前结果。

Conclusion: 该工作为一般博弈中常数遗憾问题提供了突破性解决方案，与Liu、Farina和Ozdaglar的独立工作（遗憾界为O(N²¹log⁴K)）具有相似思路但遗憾界更优。

Abstract: We introduce an uncoupled learning algorithm which, when employed by all players of an arbitrary $N$-player normal form game with up to $K$ actions per player, guarantees $O(N^3\log^2 K)$ individual regret, uniformly over the horizon of play. The proposed algorithm - which we call higher-order optimism with discounting (HOOD) is a variant of optimistic follow-the-regularized-leader (OptFTRL) that combines a discounted $(N+1)$-th order predictor with entropic regularization over a suitable "lifting" of the game's strategy space. This combination of ingredients is purposefully designed to dampen large oscillations of the induced sequence of play in a controlled manner, removing in this way a key stumbling block of previous attempts to achieve constant regret in general games. Our approach bears several striking similarities to the concurrent - and completely independent - work of Liu, Farina, and Ozdaglar (arXiv:2608.31166), who very recently derived an $O(N^{21}\log^{4} K)$ regret bound through the use of higher-order optimism and an exponential moving average estimator.

</details>


### [330] [Prospective Coding Improves Learning in Deep Continuous-Time Recurrent Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04134)
*Shivang Rawat,Mirko Morello,Flaviano Morone,David J. Heeger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出递归正交滤波器（RQFs），一种受生物学启发的复值时间滤波器（对角状态空间模型的特例），并引入参数无关的两步前瞻输入校正机制，以缓解深层连续时间循环网络中的梯度衰减问题。在多种模型和任务上验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间积分赋予连续时间循环网络记忆能力，但在深层堆叠中会延迟自下而上的信号并衰减自上而下的误差，这种失效模式亟待解决。

Method: 从能量模型出发推导RQF动力学，证明每个RQF是带通滤波器，其可学习参数控制调谐频率和带宽。通过参数无关的两步更新使每层自下而上输入具有前瞻性，不改变循环转移和并行扫描。该校正可推广至一般对角状态空间模型，并缓解时间梯度截断（仅空间反向传播）时的深度依赖梯度衰减。

Result: 在RQF、S5、ORGaNICs模型上，采用全BPTT训练时前瞻变体在所有模型和配置中均匹配或超越非前瞻对照。非残差宽度32六层RQF在raw-audio语音命令任务达96.09%准确率（31.9k参数）；宽度64六层RQF在16,384步的Path-X任务达83.56%。

Conclusion: RQFs是参数高效的循环网络基底，前瞻输入编码是深层连续时间循环网络的一种输入侧校正方法。

Abstract: Temporal integration gives continuous-time recurrent networks memory, but in deep stacks it also delays bottom-up signals and attenuates top-down errors. We develop Recursive Quadrature Filters (RQFs), biologically motivated complex-valued temporal filters that are a special case of diagonal state-space models (SSMs), and ask whether this failure mode can be addressed by making each layer's bottom-up input prospective. Starting from an energy model, we derive the RQF dynamics and show that each RQF is a band-pass filter whose learnable parameters control its tuning frequency and bandwidth. We then make each layer's bottom-up input prospective using a parameter-free two-tap update that leaves the recurrent transition and parallel scan unchanged. We extend this correction to general diagonal SSMs and show that it mitigates depth-dependent gradient attenuation when temporal gradients are truncated, i.e., spatial-only backpropagation. We evaluate the intervention in RQFs, S5, and ORGaNICs (a nonlinear gated RNN) trained using full backpropagation through time (BPTT) and spatial-only backpropagation. Under full BPTT, prospective variants match or outperform their non-prospective controls in every model and configuration. A non-residual width-32 six-layer RQF reaches 96.09% accuracy on raw-audio Speech Commands with 31.9k parameters; a width-64 six-layer RQF reaches 83.56% on the 16,384-step Path-X task. These results identify RQFs as a parameter-efficient recurrent substrate and prospective-input coding as an input-side correction for deep continuous-time recurrent networks.

</details>


### [331] [A Low-Cost, Open Platform for End-to-End Autonomous Driving on a Miniature Ackermann Vehicle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04147)
*Gustavo Claudio Karl Couto,Eric Aislan Antonelo,Gabriel George Zipperer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个低成本的开放实验平台，用于微型Ackermann车辆端到端自动驾驶研究，结合物理车辆、打印城市轨道、数据采集工具和Webots数字孪生，并实现了基于命令的行为克隆基线，验证了仿真到真实迁移的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端自动驾驶研究通常需要昂贵且难以复现的实车平台，缺乏低成本、开放、可重复的实验环境，导致仿真与真实世界之间的差距难以系统性研究。

Method: 构建包含物理微型车辆、城市轨道场景、数据采集工具、轨迹注册和Webots数字孪生的完整实验平台；实现命令条件的行为克隆（输入相机图像+导航命令，输出转向和速度）；利用数字孪生生成合成驾驶数据，并通过学习仿真到真实图像翻译器缩小外观差距，训练更高容量策略。

Result: 真实闭环实验中，学习的策略平均横向误差为6.1cm，接近人类示范的4.7cm；数字孪生中相机视场角从58度扩展到120度时，平均横向误差从35.6cm降至3.3cm；仅使用合成数据+真实数据训练的高容量策略能在所有四条赛道闭环完成，而紧凑基线和仅真实数据训练的网络完成更少。

Conclusion: 该开放平台是实用的仿真到真实（sim-to-real）研究测试平台，所提出的命令条件模仿学习基线为复现性研究提供了基础，平台已公开发布以支持可重复研究。

Abstract: This paper presents a low-cost, open experimental platform for research in end-to-end autonomous driving with miniature Ackermann vehicles. The platform combines a physical vehicle, a printed urban track, data collection tools, trajectory registration, and a Webots digital twin, enabling controlled experiments that connect simulation-based autonomous-driving methods to real-world execution. As a first baseline, we implement command-conditioned behavior cloning, in which a neural policy receives an on-board camera image and a high-level navigation command and outputs steering and speed. The system is evaluated both on the physical vehicle and in simulation. In real closed-loop experiments, the learned policy follows lanes and executes commanded turns, reaching a mean cross-track error of 6.1 cm with respect to the reference route, close to the 4.7 cm observed in human demonstrations. In the digital twin, camera field of view has a strong effect on performance, reducing the mean cross-track error from 35.6 to 3.3 cm when widened from 58 to 120 degrees. Using the digital twin to generate synthetic driving data and a learned sim-to-real image translator to reduce the appearance gap, we further show that a higher-capacity policy trained on this synthetic data combined with real demonstrations is the only configuration that completes all four track routes in closed loop, whereas the compact baseline and the same network trained on real data alone complete fewer. These results establish the open platform as a practical testbed for sim-to-real studies and provide an initial command-conditioned imitation-learning baseline; we release it to support reproducible research.

</details>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [332] [Towards Scaling Reinforcement Learning to Massive Populations: Learning Mean-Field Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02928)
*Aditya Makkar,Benjamin Unger,Jeongyeol Kwon,Mathieu Laurière,Eugene Vinitsky,Yonathan Efroni*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于表示学习的高维均值场强化学习框架。该框架假设奖励和转移动力学仅取决于种群通过一个未知的低维聚合统计量，并设计了离线学习低维表示以获取近优策略的方法。通过在供应链路由博弈上的实验验证了该方法在奖励预测和纳什均衡质量上的优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模多智能体系统（如广告拍卖、交通路由、推荐系统）中，每个智能体独立优化策略的传统方法忽视了种群动态。均值场RL虽能建模种群分布，但在大状态-动作空间或高维控制问题中，建模种群分布本身不可行。本文旨在设计一个可扩展的高维控制框架以应对大规模种群问题。

Method: 提出一个均值场RL框架，其中奖励和转移动力学仅依赖于一个未知的低维聚合统计量而非完整种群分布。在离线设置下，设计了一个可证明的方法，通过学习低维表示来获得近优策略。并设计了一个单步路由博弈来验证假设。

Result: 在固定神经网络参数数量和优化预算下，学习低维种群表示相比不利用该结构的基线方法，在奖励预测和所得策略的均衡质量（纳什间隙估计）上均有提升。

Conclusion: 利用表示学习来捕获低维种群聚合统计量是解决大规模高维均值场控制问题的有效途径，能够提升策略的学习效率与均衡质量。

Abstract: Modern multi-agent systems are increasingly deployed at scale over large populations of agents in settings such as ad-auctions, traffic routing, and recommendation systems. The dominant approach in such settings is to optimize each agent's policy independently, treating the other agents as part of a fixed single-agent environment rather than modeling the population dynamics. In many large-population systems, the dynamics depend on an aggregate summary of the population rather than the identity of any individual. Mean-field RL exploits such structure, providing a principled framework that models each agent's environment as an explicit function of the population distribution. However, in large state-action spaces or high-dimensional control problems, modeling the population distribution is itself intractable. How can we design a scalable framework for high-dimensional control problems with large populations? This work explores this question from the perspective of representation learning. We introduce a mean-field RL framework in which the rewards and transition dynamics depend on the population only through an unknown low-dimensional aggregate statistic. We then study this framework in the offline setting and design a provable approach that learns a near-optimal policy by learning a low-dimensional representation. Motivated by real-life supply-chain optimization problems, we design a one-step routing game to test the hypothesis that learning a low-dimensional population representation improves reward prediction and Nash gap estimation relative to baselines that don't exploit this structure. We show that under a fixed neural-network parameter count and optimization budget, learning a low-dimensional population representation improves reward prediction and the equilibrium quality of the resulting policies.

</details>


### [333] [LLM-Guided Reinforcement Learning for Adaptive NPC Behavior in Multi-Agent Combat Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02931)
*Hrithika Deepu Nair,Kayvan Karim*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文研究了在战斗视频游戏中，使用本地部署的Mistral 7B大语言模型作为运行时策略选择器，引导共享PPO策略的NPC智能体。通过在Unity中训练5个NPC并对比基线配置与LLM增强配置，在600个回合中对抗三种脚本对手进行评估，结果显示LLM增强的智能体在对抗Balanced对手时胜率从11%提升至24%，但策略选择分析显示83.8%的情况选择Surround，表明该模型规模的零样本策略区分能力有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脚本化NPC行为可预测易被利用，而RL智能体训练后策略固定，无法根据不同对手灵活调整策略。作者旨在探索一种运行时策略选择框架，在不修改底层策略的前提下，利用LLM引导已训练的RL策略，实现自适应的多智能体游戏AI。

Method: 训练5个共享PPO策略的NPC智能体，对比两种配置：（1）基线配置，策略独立行动；（2）LLM增强配置，通过Ollama访问本地Mistral 7B，每5秒读取实时游戏状态并分配四种战术标签之一。在Unity中针对三种脚本化对手（Balanced、Evasive、Aggressive）进行600个回合评估，使用Mann-Whitney U检验分析结果，并分析了2430次策略选择的分布。

Result: 对抗Balanced对手时胜率从11%翻倍至24%且回合时长显著增加；对抗Evasive对手时胜率和击杀速度提升但回合时长不符合严格假设；对抗Aggressive对手时LLM频繁选择包围战术反而不利。策略选择分析显示Surround占83.8%，与对手类型无关，表明模型规模的零样本策略区分能力有限。

Conclusion: LLM引导的运行时策略选择在自适应多智能体游戏AI方面展示了潜力，尤其能提升应对动态变换策略对手的表现；但同时也暴露出局限性，提示在更大模型规模或更精细的提示设计下可能获得更好的策略区分度。

Abstract: Scripted and rule-based non-player characters (NPCs) in combat video games often exhibit predictable behaviors that experienced players can exploit, while reinforcement learning (RL) agents typically retain a fixed policy after training and cannot readily adapt their strategy to different opponents. We investigate a runtime strategy-selection framework in which a large language model (LLM) guides a trained RL policy without modifying its underlying behavior. To demonstrate this, we train five NPC agents with a shared PPO policy in Unity and compare a baseline configuration, in which the policy acts independently, with an LLM-augmented configuration in which a locally hosted Mistral 7B model, accessed through Ollama, reads the live game state every five seconds and assigns one of four tactical tags. We evaluate both configurations against three scripted opponent types across 600 episodes and analyze outcomes using the Mann-Whitney U test. Against a Balanced opponent that changes tactics during an episode, the LLM-augmented agents more than doubled their win rate from 11% to 24% and produced significantly longer episodes. Against an Evasive opponent, the augmented agents achieved a higher win rate and faster kills, although their shorter episode duration did not satisfy the strict hypothesis definition. Against an Aggressive opponent, the LLM's near-constant preference for encirclement was counterproductive. Analysis of 2,430 strategy selections showed that Surround was selected in 83.8% of cases regardless of opponent type, indicating limited zero-shot strategic differentiation at this model scale. These results demonstrate both the potential and limitations of LLM-guided runtime strategy selection for adaptive multi-agent game AI.

</details>


### [334] [You Can't Escape Your Own Activations : Evaluation Awareness and Multi-Agent Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03035)
*Aritra Das,Jaee Ponde,Mihir More,Debayan Gupta*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本研究探讨当智能体明确知道自己内部激活被监控并收到监控反馈时，基于激活的探针检测多智能体串通行为的性能是否会下降。结果表明，告知智能体监控存在并不会降低最佳探针的检测准确性，智能体仍然继续串通。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在多智能体系统中可能串通，输出监控器易被混淆和隐写术欺骗，因此需要基于内部激活的探针。但现有探针评估通常假设智能体不知道自己被监控，这不符合实际部署场景。

Method: 在固定模型、探针和阈值不变的情况下，仅改变智能体被告知的信息（无信息基线、告知有激活监控存在、告知监控存在并附带上一轮得分反馈），在四人二十一点游戏和两人Simmons囚徒博弈中，使用Qwen3-32B-AWQ和GPT-OSS-20B进行同质和混合配对测试。

Result: 告知智能体监控存在并不会让它们成功隐藏串通行为。最佳探针在所有三种条件下都保持准确，且智能体在被告知监控后仍继续串通。

Conclusion: 基于激活的检测方法在面对知晓监控的智能体时依然具有鲁棒性，为多智能体系统中的串通检测提供了可靠保障。

Abstract: LLM agents are increasingly deployed in multi-agent systems, where they can collude while keeping their actions benign. Output monitors designed to detect such collusions can be fooled by obfuscation and steganography, motivating the use of probes trained on internal activations. However, these probes are usually evaluated on agents that do not know they are being watched. We study how activation-based detection changes when agents are explicitly informed that their internal activations are being monitored, and when they additionally receive feedback from the monitor. We keep the models, probes, and thresholds fixed and change only what the agents are told: nothing (baseline), that an activation monitor is present (aware), or that a monitor is present together with the previous round's score (feedback). We test two games, a four-agent blackjack game and a two-agent Simmons prisoners game, using Qwen3-32B-AWQ and GPT-OSS-20B in homogeneous and mixed pairings. Telling agents about the monitor does not hide them. The best probes stay accurate in all three conditions, and the agents keep colluding.

</details>


### [335] [Where Reliability Lives: Experimental Localisation of Behavioural Properties in an Agent System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03192)
*Timothy Marsden,Matthew Collecutt,James Marsden*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个模拟定居点系统，将心智、制度与世界相分离，通过预先注册的实验证明：某些行为属性（如接受现实唯一性、拒绝无效行为、职责延续等）在认知被大幅改变（包括替换为前沿LLM面板）时仍保持稳定，即这些属性定位在制度机制而非模型本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关于智能体系统可靠性的论断通常隐含地假设属性存在于模型的某处或外围机制中，但很少有研究将这种"定位"作为可实验检验的问题。作者旨在通过设计一个可分离心智、制度和世界三个层次的系统，来确定行为属性的真正归属位置。

Method: 构建一个持续性模拟定居点，采用仅追加的分类账本作为权威记录来裁决所有行为。实验分两方向：(1)固定认知不变，干预制度的认识论机制（证据溯源、信念可得性、物理证据可读性），并进行预先注册的预测检验；(2)固定制度不变，以四种方式干预认知（消融原生心智机制、中途杀死并重置、用冻结的前沿LLM面板替换全部认知、注入受信任的虚假证词）。

Result: 预先注册的中心预测被两次驳斥且方向相反；在加入经过验证的一手证人后，其边际价值从非正转为正（11个种子中9个有效，零虚假归因）。认知干预方面，单一虚假信息使信任臂消耗约900次无效行为，不信任臂为零。五个预先声明的属性在所有测试轨迹中均未改变：接受现实保持唯一、无效尝试被带类型理由拒绝、职责超出进程存续、无工作被重复接受、无虚假完成被接受（2,581个替换面板声明无一虚假）。

Conclusion: 该研究的结论局限于此特定设定：通过干预证明，所测量的行为属性可以与认知的实质性变化相分离。这是一个设计的世界而非制度群体，且未测试针对制度进行优化的智能体。

Abstract: Reliability claims about agentic systems implicitly locate each property somewhere: in the model, or in the machinery around it. We built a system where that location is an experimental question. The subject is a persistent simulated settlement whose authoritative append-only ledger adjudicates every attempted act against world state; accepted history is the only reality. Mind, institution and world were separated before any experiment. Holding cognition fixed, we intervened on the institution's epistemic mechanisms (evidence provenance, belief availability, physical-evidence legibility); preregistered experiments refuted our central prediction twice, in opposite directions. A registered falsifier then supplied the input the geometry had denied the belief channel, a staged veridical first-hand witness, and its marginal value, non-positive throughout the witness-free phases, turned positive: 9 of 11 seeds, zero added false attribution. Holding institutional enforcement fixed, we intervened on cognition four ways: ablating the native minds' machinery, killing and resetting them mid-task, substituting a frozen frontier-LLM panel for the entire native cognition, and corrupting beliefs with trusted false testimony. Behaviour changed dramatically: one falsehood cost each trusting run about 900 futile actions and the distrusting arm none. Five pre-declared properties did not move in any tested trajectory: accepted reality stayed singular, invalid attempts were refused with typed reasons, duties outlived their processes, no work was accepted twice, and no false completion was ever accepted (2,581 substituted-panel claims, none false). Our claim is limited to this setting: measured behavioural properties were separable from substantial changes to cognition, established by intervention. One designed world, not a population of institutions; no test of an agent optimising against the institution.

</details>


### [336] [The Civilization Framework: Sovereign-Anchored Communication Between Personal Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03425)
*Guangjun Liu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出Civilization框架和Embassy协议，将可寻址实体从单个代理提升至"文明"层面（一个人类主权者、持久账本和可互换代理），解决AI系统间通信中人类作为传输层导致上下文丢失的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类在AI系统之间充当传输层，每次跳转都会丢失上下文；现有代理级寻址无法解决多代理协作中的通信完整性和权威性问题。

Method: 提出Civilization Framework（以文明为寻址单位，包含人类主权者、持久账本和可互换代理）和Embassy Protocol（运营商无关的叠加协议，消息异步到达驻留账本端点，以双方账本上的承诺状态而非投递作为最终真值）。通过预注册的1,908次试验实验测试时间加权效应。

Result: 发现时间加权效应：移除验证后，不正确的上游声明先到达时捕获54.2%的答案（完全验证下为4.2%）；接收者封存自己答案后到达的相同声明捕获31.6%。结果因工具使用检查未满足调用预算条件而被归类为探索性发现。

Conclusion: 时间加权效应是AI间通信中的真实风险；先到达的信息可能获得不应有的权威性。协议验证机制能显著缓解该风险，但需要进一步实验验证（含工具使用的复制实验计划中）。框架的文明内层已有可运行实现。

Abstract: Humans are the transport layer between AI systems, losing context at every hop. We present the Civilization Framework, whose addressable party is the civilization, not the agent (one human sovereign, a persistent ledger, and interchangeable agents), and the Embassy Protocol, a carrier-agnostic overlay: messages arrive asynchronously at a resident ledger endpoint, any online agent of the receiver handles them, and commitment state on both ledgers, not delivery, is ground truth. Authority derives from memory: an agent's power to act for its civilization is capped by the memory it can access and externalized through signed credentials, separate from civilization-level reputation. We identify the temporal-weight effect, a hazard in AI-to-AI communication where what arrives first acquires unearned authority, and test it in one frontier model in a preregistered 1,908-trial experiment. With verification removed, an incorrect upstream claim arriving first captures 54.2% of answers (4.2% under full verification), while the same claim arriving after the receiver has sealed its own answer captures 31.6% (the two prompt shells are not length-matched, so part of that gap may reflect shell form; see Section 7), and both registered question-set specifications agree on these two verdicts (the exclusion specification is preregistered as under-powered). Two secondary results, the mitigation from instruction-level provenance labeling and sealed-answer accuracy equivalence, are specification-dependent, holding only under the all-questions specification. Because a registered check of tool use failed its call-budget condition, the registration classifies the round as inconclusive and every result above, primary and secondary, is reported as exploratory; a replication with harness-enforced budgets is planned. The framework's intra-civilization layer has a working implementation.

</details>
